### [1] [Horizon mergers and initial test-charge acceleration in charged black-hole geometries with anisotropic sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28095)
*Saeed Noori Gashti,Mohammad Ali S. Afshar,Mohammad Reza Alipour*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究在带弦云、Kiselev幂律或对数分布等静态球对称黑洞背景中，视界合并条件与带电测试粒子的初始径向加速度，给出局域荷质比阈值及若干精确数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的有效环境应力张量（弦云、Kiselev幂律、对数分布）是各向异性的，不能简单等同于均匀暗能量（quintessence）或无压冷暗物质。作者希望在一个统一的质量函数框架下，厘清这类黑洞的视界合并条件及其对带电测试粒子动力学的影响，并明确结论的适用范围（非两黑洞力律、非宇宙加速机制、非弱引力猜想检验）。

Method: 分离麦克斯韦贡献后，将有效环境应力张量视为各向异性源；采用统一的质量函数形式推导视界合并条件并分类 Kiselev 分支；对相对静态观测者从静止释放的带电测试粒子，推导其向外初始加速所需的局域荷质比阈值，并结合密度匹配给出条件性的分布归一化。

Result: 在Kiselev族中，内-事件视界合并电荷仅给出上界，三个正视界还可能要求一个与事件-宇宙学合并相关的正电荷下界。对次极端的弦云情形，荷质比阈值 λ=q/μ 的下确界为 1/z（z=Q/Q_ext），仅在无穷远处趋近；对数分布情形的阈值在整个有限外部区域超过1并取得有限全局最小值，例如 α/M=0.4、Q/M=1.2 时 λ_*≈1.251125。非简并Kiselev静态块中的中性平衡半径唯一且径向不稳定。

Conclusion: 这些结果刻画了该族黑洞的几何视界界限与测试粒子动力学，但不构成两黑洞力律或宇宙加速机制，也不构成对弱引力猜想的检验。

Abstract: We study horizon mergers and the initial radial acceleration of charged test particles in static, spherically symmetric black-hole geometries with a string cloud, a Kiselev power-law component, or a logarithmic profile conventionally. After separating the Maxwell contribution, the effective environmental stress tensors are anisotropic and are not identified with homogeneous quintessence or pressureless cold dark matter. Using a common mass-function formulation, we derive the horizon-merger conditions and classify the Kiselev branches. In this family, the inner-event merger charge provides only an upper bound: three distinct positive horizons can additionally require a positive lower charge bound associated with the event-cosmological merger. For a test particle released from rest relative to a static observer, we derive the local charge-to-mass threshold for outward initial acceleration. Writing $λ=q/μ$ and $z=Q/Q_{\rm ext}$, where $Q_{\rm ext}$ is the inner-event merger charge, the subextremal string-cloud threshold has infimum $1/z$, approached only at infinity. For the subextremal logarithmic profile, the threshold exceeds unity throughout the finite exterior and attains a finite global minimum. For $α/M=0.4$ and $Q/M=1.2$, this minimum is $λ_*\simeq1.251125$. In a nondegenerate Kiselev static patch, the neutral balance radius is unique and radially unstable. Density matching supplies conditional profile normalizations, not observational fits. For the logarithmic profile, the integration parameter is distinguished from the mass combination inferred from circular motion. These results characterize geometric horizon bounds and test-particle dynamics; they do not establish a two-black-hole force law or a mechanism for cosmic acceleration, nor do they constitute a test of the Weak Gravity Conjecture.

</details>


### [2] [Level repulsion and the quantum viscosity of near-extremal black branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28203)
*Panos Betzios,Olga Papadoulaki,Maria Myrto Pegioudi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究近极值黑洞膜在AdS₂喉道尺度以下的剪切粘滞，发现微扰计算使η/s发散，通过将JT引力补全为矩阵积分（非微扰完备化）后，能级排斥使直流粘滞消失，并在双尺度极限下获得有限且普适的粘滞；在极低温度下问题约化为Airy核，淬灭平均的η/s在所有非零温度下有限为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS₂喉道的能隙尺度以下，量子涨落使JT引力盘近似中的剪切粘滞按(CT)^{-1/2}增长，单圈η/s发散。需要引入JT引力的非微扰完备化（矩阵积分）来消除发散，并理解极低温度下粘滞与熵的真正行为。

Method: 将JT引力非微扰完备化为随机矩阵积分（矩阵模型），利用Kubo公式探测矩阵谱的能级关联普适特征；在有限极值熵S₀下分析能级排斥，并在双尺度极限（探测频率ω与能级间距尺度ω_sp≡e^{-S₀}/C保持固定比例）下计算Schwarzian增长饱和；极低温度CT∼e^{-2S₀/3}下借助谱边缘的Airy核精确求解，并与由弦方程（string equation）定义、谱有下界的显式完备化进行比较。

Result: 有限S₀时能级排斥使直流（DC）粘滞为零；在双尺度极限下存在有限普适粘滞，固定ω且ω_sp≪ω≪e^{S₀/3}ω_sp时Schwarzian增长饱和于η_max≃0.174(ω/ω_sp)η_sc；极低温度CT∼e^{-2S₀/3}时属展开与单圈熵计算完全失效，问题精确约化为Airy核，淬灭平均的η与s之比在所有非零温度下有限且为正；在弦方程定义的显式完备化中，体结果相同，淬灭谱边缘结果仅相差百分之几，而退火量对非微扰完备化有一阶依赖。

Conclusion: 非微扰完备化可以消除剪切粘滞η/s的发散并揭示普适的能级关联物理：能级排斥消除DC粘滞，双尺度极限下出现有限普适粘滞；极低温度下精确的Airy核描述给出正且有限的淬灭η/s；体结果对完备化不敏感，但退火量敏感依赖非微扰完备化。

Abstract: Below the gap scale of the AdS$_2$ throat, quantum fluctuations make the shear viscosity of near-extremal black branes grow as $(CT)^{-1/2}$ in the disk approximation of JT gravity, and the one-loop $η/s$ diverges. To cure this divergence, we include the non-perturbative completion of JT gravity as a matrix integral. The Kubo formula then probes universal features of level correlations. At finite extremal entropy $S_0$, level repulsion makes the DC viscosity vanish. A finite, universal viscosity survives in a double-scaling limit in which the probe frequency $ω$ is held in a fixed ratio to the level-spacing scale $ω_{\rm sp}\equiv e^{-S_0}/C$. At fixed $ω$, with $ω_{\rm sp}\llω\ll e^{S_0/3}ω_{\rm sp}$ the Schwarzian growth then saturates at $η_{\max}\simeq0.174\,(ω/ω_{\rm sp})\,η_{sc}$, where $η_{sc}$ is the semiclassical viscosity, and the viscosity decreases at lower temperatures. At the extremely low temperature scale $CT\sim e^{-2S_0/3}$ the genus expansion and the one-loop entropy computations completely break down. There we start probing the edge of the spectrum and the problem reduces exactly to the Airy kernel. We find that the ratio of the quenched averages of $η$ and $s$ is finite and positive at every non-zero temperature. We compare our results with explicit completions defined by the string equation, in which the spectrum is bounded from below. The bulk results are the same in these completions and the quenched edge results change only by a few per cent, while the annealed quantities depend on the non-perturbative completion at order one.

</details>


### [3] [The index from a super-point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28204)
*Dhanvarsh Annamalai,Hyunook Hur,Thomas Hussel,Monica Jinwoo Kang,Craig Lawrie,Jaewon Song*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将用于编码4d N=2超共形场论Higgs分支与Macdonald指标的滤波分级环的代数几何构造，推广到Schur sector含费米子生成元的情形，此时环为Z2-分级交换，指标由广义弧空间（定义理想可含生成元及其导数间的关系）的Hilbert级数再现。作者研究了多个具体理论并检验了构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作给出纯玻色情形下，通过滤波分级环/弧空间几何编码4d N=2 SCFT的Higgs分支与Macdonald指标。本文旨在将这一构造推广至Schur sector包含费米子生成元的理论，从而得到Z2-分级交换环，并用广义弧空间（其定义理想需纳入生成元及其导数间的关系）的Hilbert级数来复现Macdonald指标。

Method: 作者将环推广为Z2-分级交换，引入广义弧空间概念，允许定义理想包含生成元及其导数间的非平凡关系。对以下理论逐一构造并检验：自由N=2矢量多重态、规范代数su(2)与su(3)的N=4超Yang-Mills、秩1的N=3 SCFT，以及E6帽子(SU(2)) SCFT。对秩1 N=3理论，理想由一个超势及其两个超对称变分的Jacobi理想之和给出；对N=4 SYM，理想为单个N=4超场各分量的Jacobi理想之和。对E6帽子理论，考察其相关VOA即Feigin-Feigin-Tipunin A(6)代数及其Zhu C2代数的弧空间，并补充一阶jet环中的三个关系。

Result: 对秩1的N=3 SCFT，理想等于单个超势及其两个超对称变分的Jacobi理想之和；对N=4 SYM，理想同样为单个N=4超场分量（对su(2)是su(2)味对称的单重态，对su(3)是三重态）的Jacobi理想之和。E6帽子(SU(2))理论的关联VOA为A(6)代数；其Zhu C2代数的弧空间不能复现真空特征标，故A(6)不是经典自由的。通过在Zhu C2代数上补充一阶jet环中的三个关系，广义弧空间至少在q^51阶以内复现了E6帽子(SU(2))的Macdonald指标。

Conclusion: 将滤波分级环与弧空间构造推广到含费米子生成元的理论是可行的：通过Z2-分级交换环及允许含导数关系的广义弧空间，可成功编码并复现所研究各理论的Macdonald指标。对N=3与N=4情形，理想具有源于超势/超场Jacobi理想之和的统一结构；对E6帽子(SU(2))，其VOA A(6)并非经典自由，需在一阶jet环补充三个关系后广义弧空间才能（至少到q^51阶）重现Macdonald指标。

Abstract: We extend the algebro-geometric construction of filtered graded rings that encode both the Higgs branch and the Macdonald index of 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theories to theories whose Schur sector contains fermionic generators. The rings are then $\mathbb{Z}_2$-graded-commutative, and the index is reproduced by the Hilbert series of a generalized arc space, whose defining ideal may contain relations among the generators and their derivatives. We study the free $\mathcal{N}=2$ vector multiplet, $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills with gauge algebras $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ and $\mathfrak{su}(3)$, the rank-one $\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFTs, and the $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ SCFT. For the rank-one $\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFTs, the ideal is the sum of the Jacobian ideals of a single superpotential and of its two supersymmetry variations. For $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills, the ideal is similarly the sum of the Jacobian ideals of the components of a single $\mathcal{N}=4$ superfield, which is a singlet of the $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ flavor symmetry for gauge algebra $\mathfrak{su}(2)$, and a triplet for $\mathfrak{su}(3)$. The associated VOA for the $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ theory is given by the Feigin--Feigin--Tipunin $\mathcal{A}(6)$ algebra. The arc space of Zhu's $C_2$-algebra of $\mathcal{A}(6)$ does not reproduce the vacuum character, and therefore $\mathcal{A}(6)$ is not classically free. Supplementing Zhu's $C_2$-algebra by three relations in the first jet ring, the generalized arc space reproduces the Macdonald index of $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ up to, at least, order $q^{51}$.

</details>


### [4] [The Gradient Structure of Perturbative RG Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28205)
*Lorenzo Benfatto,William H. Pannell,Andreas Stergiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明，通过球面上的共形Ward恒等式，可以通过方案选择消除Jack–Osborn 35余年来未能消除的反对称度量贡献，使重整化群β函数成为关于对称度量的势A的梯度流（微扰论所有阶成立）。由此在固定点A=sin(πd/2)ln Z_{S^d}，推出微扰流的F-定理；相关算符源（与曲率混合）不影响该结果，唯一例外是自由标量场源在d=6,10,...附近，故d=6附近的φ³理论的梯度性质仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重整化群流在2、4、6维的Weyl一致性条件表明其几乎为梯度流，但阻碍是耦合常数空间度量上的反对称贡献。该阻碍自Jack和Osborn 35余年前提出以来一直未能通过方案选择消除。本文旨在消除此反对称贡献，从而将β函数表为势的梯度，并进而建立F-定理。

Method: 利用球面S^d上的共形Ward恒等式，证明反对称度量贡献可通过重整化方案（coupling redefinition/scheme choice）的选择被移除，使β函数在微扰论所有阶、任意维d、有无Weyl反常的理论中均为对称度量下势A的梯度。领头阶度量由扰动算符的两点函数确定，酉性保证弱耦合下正定。

Result: 得到β函数为梯度流：β = g·∇A（对称度量）；固定点处A = F̃ = sin(πd/2) ln Z_{S^d}，从而推出微扰流所有圈阶的F̃-定理。相关算符源（在球上与曲率混合）不影响结论；酉理论中唯一例外是自由标量场源在d=6,10,...附近，故d=6附近的φ³理论梯度性质仍是开放的。

Conclusion: 通过在球面上运用共形Ward恒等式，可消除长期存在的反对称度量阻碍，使重整化群流成为严格梯度流，并据此证明F̃-定理对微扰流的普适性。结果在任意维、有无Weyl反常的理论中成立；d=6附近的φ³理论因自由标量场例外情形而保持开放。

Abstract: Weyl consistency conditions show that perturbative renormalisation group flows in two, four and six dimensions are almost gradient flows. The obstruction is an antisymmetric contribution to the metric on the space of couplings, which has resisted removal since the work of Jack and Osborn more than 35 years ago. We show that a conformal Ward identity on the sphere allows it to be removed by a choice of scheme. The beta function is then the gradient of a potential $A$ with respect to a symmetric metric, to all orders in perturbation theory. The Ward identity gives this result in any dimension $d$, in theories with and without a Weyl anomaly. At leading order the metric is fixed by the two-point functions of the perturbing operators, so unitarity makes it positive definite at weak coupling. At fixed points $A$ equals $\widetilde{F}=\sin\tfrac{πd}{2}\,\ln Z_{S^{d}}$, so the $\widetilde{F}$-theorem follows for perturbative flows to all loop orders. We show that sources of relevant operators, which on the sphere mix with the curvature, do not affect these results. In unitary theories the only exception is the source of a free scalar field near $d=6,10,\ldots\,$, so that for $φ^3$ theory near six dimensions the gradient property remains open.

</details>


### [5] [An all-orders proof of gradient flow for multiscalar $φ^4$ theory in four dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28206)
*Marten Reehorst*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明四维多标量φ⁴理论的重整化群流在微扰论所有阶上都是梯度流，并验证了相关的六圈关系式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度流性质（RG流可写为某个势函数的梯度）对理解重整化群的结构、固定点的稳定性以及C定理等具有重要意义，但此前只在低圈或特定条件下成立。作者旨在将其推广到微扰论所有阶，并厘清Osborn-Jack Weyl相容条件中反称张量部分的贡献。

Method: 利用Osborn和Jack的Weyl相容条件，假设微扰论中局域耦合的生成泛函有限、且大动量下大质量Feynman积分的已知展开式在每一动量处余项有界。先由相容条件得到含反称部分的梯度公式；再对外场转动流和赝质量算符源，导出远离固定点、常耦合下边缘算符两点函数的Ward恒等式，将一项反称贡献改写为两点函数与三点函数一阶矩的表达式；随后证明改写后各项均等价于度规的对称贡献与势的贡献，并给出显式形式，且度规在所有阶正定。

Result: 证明了四维多标量φ⁴理论的RG流在微扰论所有阶都是梯度流；度规到所有阶正定；特别推出Pannell和Stergiou在六圈得到的四个五、六圈外场转动系数关系式（作为梯度流的条件），并通过显式计算验证这些关系式确实成立。

Conclusion: 多标量φ⁴理论四维的RG流在所有阶均为梯度流，反称项经Ward恒等式改写后可吸收为度规的对称部分和势的贡献，度规正定，从而严格支持该理论的梯度流结构。

Abstract: We prove that the renormalisation group flow of multiscalar $φ^4$ theory in four dimensions is a gradient flow to all orders in perturbation theory. The proof uses the Weyl consistency conditions of Osborn and Jack, and it assumes two properties of perturbation theory: that the generating functional with local couplings is finite (also assumed by Osborn and Jack), and that the known expansion of massive Feynman integrals at large momenta holds with a bound on its remainder at each momentum. The consistency conditions give a gradient formula in which the tensor that multiplies the beta function is allowed to have an antisymmetric part, determined by coefficients of the Weyl anomaly. We derive a Ward identity for the two-point function of the marginal operators at constant couplings away from fixed points, with sources for the currents of field rotations and for the mass operator. It re-expresses one of the anti-symmetric contributions in terms of two-point functions and the first moment of the three-point function. We show that, after this rewrite, each of the resulting terms is equivalent to a symmetric contribution to the metric and a contribution to the potential, and we give both explicitly. The metric is positive definite to all orders in perturbation theory. The proof implies in particular the four relations among five- and six-loop coefficients of the field rotation, which Pannell and Stergiou derived as the conditions for a gradient flow at six loops. We check by explicit computation that these relations indeed hold. A companion paper treats $d=4-\varepsilon$ and other critical dimensions with a Ward identity on the sphere.

</details>

*Csaba Csáki,Larissa Kiriliuk,Zhihan Liu,Rotem Ovadia*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清't Hooft-Polyakov磁单极场中Rubakov-Callan催化质子衰变的微观机制，特别是为何其速率不受真空瞬子那种指数压低，并理解绕数与费米子零模之间的拓扑关系。

Method: 作者构造并研究了“带源的SU(2)瞬子”——一种由费米子源承载、携带单位瞬子数的拓扑规范构型；通过分析其绕数与费米子零模数目之间的对应关系，提出带源瞬子的指标定理，并计算磁单极核心内外费米子插入的领头阶关联函数的闭式表达式。

Result: 带源SU(2)瞬子的速率不受指数压低（不同于真空BPST瞬子）；绕数与费米子零模数目一致，支持带源瞬子指标定理的存在；获得了核心内外费米子插入领头阶关联函数的解析形式。

Conclusion: Rubakov-Callan催化本质上是一种红外效应：散射费米子并未到达磁单极核心，质子衰变经由带源SU(2)瞬子实现，从而解释了其非指数压低的衰变速率。

Abstract: We show that Rubakov-Callan catalysis of proton decay in the field of a 't Hooft-Polyakov monopole proceeds through "sourced SU(2) instantons", which are novel topological gauge configurations sourced by fermions and carrying unit instanton number. Unlike vacuum BPST instantons, these sourced instantons do not give rise to exponentially suppressed rates. We relate the winding number to the number of fermionic zero modes, suggesting an underlying index theorem for sourced instantons. We also calculate the closed-form expression for the leading-order correlator for fermion insertions both outside and inside the monopole core. Our results indicate that Rubakov-Callan catalysis is an IR effect where the scattering fermions do not reach the monopole core.

</details>

Abstract: We revisit non-relativistic quantum-mechanical scattering in a Coulomb potential. We construct a new set of canonical variables through a time-dependent canonical transformation that removes the logarithmic Coulomb correction from the asymptotic phase-space coordinates. Using these variables, we construct the corresponding Heisenberg S-matrix through several complementary approaches. This leads to a formulation of the Lehmann--Symanzik--Zimmermann (LSZ) reduction formalism adapted to the modified asymptotic dynamics, which we relate to the Dollard scattering operator. Result: 无化学势时，精确系数与晚期估计之差为仅依赖圈内质量和软顶点耦合的普适因子；对重标准模型圈，精确信号比晚期估计大十至三十倍；有化学势时比值依赖模型，在基准点上螺旋矢量达到三十倍、费米子三角约一百倍（与近期精确结果一致）；偏离压缩极限时领头项很快失效，主要是振荡相位的漂移，并对标量和费米子圈导出了该漂移。

Conclusion: 精确计算显著修正了晚期时间估计的结果，信号幅度普遍被放大，且偏离压缩极限的行为由相位漂移主导，表明在暴胀原初关联函数中采用精确而非近似处理至关重要。

Abstract: Fermions and massive fields charged under an unbroken symmetry couple to the inflaton only in pairs, so their signals in primordial correlators begin at one loop. Templates for these signals are mostly late-time estimates. Starting from a representation of massive de Sitter correlators as finite Mellin-Barnes integrals, we compute the exact squeezed coefficients of these signals and their corrections away from the squeezed limit. The leading coefficient factorizes into a hard part and a soft vertex. Late-time estimates treat the hard part exactly but approximate the soft vertex by its late-time limit, whereas we compute both exactly. Without a chemical potential, the exact coefficient differs from the late-time one by a universal factor that depends only on the masses in the loop and the coupling at the soft vertex. For the heavy Standard Model loops, the exact signals are ten to thirty times larger than their late-time estimates. With a chemical potential, the ratio depends on the model, and at our benchmarks it reaches a factor of thirty for helical vectors and about a hundred for the fermion triangle, where we agree with recent exact results. Away from the squeezed limit the leading term soon fails, mainly through a drift in the phase of the oscillation, which we derive for scalar and fermion loops.

</details>

Abstract: This work is inspired by the nonabelianization construction of Virasoro conformal blocks. This generalized nonabelianization map uses the datum of a $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ spectral network on $C$ and we find that this map also depends on the soliton degeneracies corresponding to the spectral network even though the soliton data didn't show up explicitly in the Virasoro case. We provide a check of the proposed construction associated to the more challenging $(123)$ type branch points which start to show up in three-fold covers. Abstract: We investigate superconducting proximity effects in NbN/Pt/Py and NbN/Cu/Py trilayers using broadband ferromagnetic resonance (FMR). The reduction of the spin-pumping contribution to the Py damping below the superconducting transition temperature (Tc) is used to probe the superconducting correlations and electronic density of state in the buried metallic interlayer. For a Cu interlayer, the damping reduction remains finite up to at least 20 nm, in agreement with the large coherence length above 100nm in Cu. In contrast, the damping reduction for a Pt interlayer exhibits a non-monotonic thickness dependence and is fully suppressed above 5 nm. Using the quasiclassical theory, we are able to explain the oscillation in damping reduction below Tc with the coexistence of superconducting and magnetic proximity effects in Pt, which generates oscillations of the superconducting order parameter and electronic density of state in the exchange-splitted Pt interlayer. Our results establish spin-pumping damping as a sensitive probe of superconducting proximity effects in buried metallic layers.

Abstract: Superconducting vortex represents a topological entity in type-II superconductors, where the prototypical manifestation of such a macroscopic quantum state holds great promise for applications in future quantum technologies. However, it is still scientifically challenging due to the intrinsic nature of the vortex pinning landscapes in superconductors. Here, we observe an even-in-field Hall-like signal with about two order of magnitude enhancement in a type-II superconductor/chiral magnet heterostructure, which confines to the field-temperature window where the flux flow coexists with magnetic skyrmions. The polarity of such a giant even-in-field transverse response reverses between orthogonal current directions, suggesting an on-track vortex flow driven by a nematic fluxon-skyrmion interaction analyzed via the extended vortex-viscosity tensor. More critically, a deterministic manipulation of skyrmions at the nanoscale within the hybrid system enables selective and reversible controls over the dissipative behavior and therefore the reconfigurable fluxon transport, mimic the precise control of curling stone in winter Olympic, thereby opening a new avenue towards the exploration of programmable topological and superconducting quantum devices.

Abstract: In our recent work (Phys. Rev. B \textbf{113}, 224503 (2026)), we showed numerically that rapidly sweeping the bias current of a current-biased Josephson junction threshold detector (CBJJ-JTD) drives it out of equilibrium. The scheme requires very low damping ($β\leq 10^{-4}$) and a very fast bias-current sweep ($v/β\geq 1$), both of which are difficult to achieve with available junctions and low-pass-filtered cryogenic measurement lines. The applied flux drives a circulating supercurrent and sustains nonequilibrium phase dynamics throughout the bias-current sweep, removing the need for a fast sweep or for transient oscillations set by the initial phase. Using typical experimental device parameters, we calculate switching-current distributions (SCDs) with and without a microwave input for both driven and undriven SQUIDs. We quantify detection performance using receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curves and their area under the curve (AUC). The simulations show that high-frequency flux drive reduces the impact of thermal noise and enables statistical discrimination of weak microwave pulses at the single-photon energy scale. We also numerically estimate detector characteristics such as phase selectivity and dynamic range.

Method: 提出缺失耦合来自电荷歧化母相有序矢量Q=(π,π,π)/a处的动力学电荷涨落，引入过阻尼玻色子模型描述之；利用x=0.4密度泛函计算导带能带的紧束缚拟合，解析推导自能、准粒子权重与能带重构（含影子带和热线赝能隙）；结合LDA声子与频率ωm≈25meV、耦合λ≈0.7的过阻尼模式做各向异性Eliashberg计算；并在扩展Hubbard模型的随机相近似下研究该玻色子的自然产生。

Result: 该玻色子仅强烈散射费米面上沿⟨111⟩方向的小环（热线，k与k+Q皆在费米面上）上的电子；求和规则固定了费米面平均耦合，与玻色子频率和关联长度无关；各向异性Eliashberg计算在关联长度为2–3个晶格常数时复现Tc≈25–30K，并预言热环上能隙增大3–4倍；较长关联长度下s波态与局限于热线的变号T2g态紧密竞争；纯电子涨落太弱且过长程，与呼吸模式晶格畸变的耦合是增强它们的一种可能途径。

Abstract: The origin of superconductivity near 30~K in Ba$_{0.6}$K$_{0.4}$BiO$_3$ has remained a long-standing puzzle. Tunneling experiments give an electron--boson coupling $λ\approx 0.7$--1.2, whereas density-functional calculations give $λ\approx 0.3$, concentrated in the breathing mode near 50--70~meV. We propose that the missing coupling of about 0.7 comes from dynamical charge fluctuations at Q=(pi,pi,pi)/a, the ordering vector of the charge-disproportionated parent compound, and introduce an overdamped boson model to describe them. Using a tight-binding fit to the conduction band found from density-functional calculations at $x=0.4$, we show that such a boson scatters electrons strongly only on hot lines of the Fermi surface, small loops around the $\langle 111\rangle$ directions where k and k+Q are both at the Fermi level. We derive closed-form self-energies, quasiparticle residues and band reconstructions, including shadow bands and hot-line pseudogaps for long-range correlations, and show that a sum rule fixes the Fermi-surface-averaged coupling independently of the boson frequency and correlation length. Combining the LDA phonon with an overdamped mode of frequency $ω_m\approx 25$~meV and coupling $λ\approx 0.7$, anisotropic Eliashberg calculations reproduce $T_c\approx 25$--30~K for correlation lengths of two to three lattice spacings and predict a gap enhanced by a factor of 3--4 on the hot loops. For longer correlation lengths an $s$-wave state competes closely with a sign-changing $T_{2g}$ state confined to the hot loops. In the random-phase approximation for an extended Hubbard model, the boson emerges naturally near a charge instability at $R$, but purely electronic fluctuations are too weak and too long-ranged; coupling to the breathing-mode lattice distortion is one possible way to strengthen them.

Abstract: We investigate the normal-state electrical properties of ultrathin amorphous Mo$_{1-x}$Si$_x$ films deposited by DC magnetron co-sputtering over a broad range of compositions and thicknesses. Films with Si contents of $x = 0.14$ to $0.5$ were deposited on Si/SiO$_2$ and Si/Si$_3$N$_4$ substrates (monocrystalline Si with SiO$_2$ and S$_3$N$_4$ buffer layers respectfully); X-ray diffraction confirms an amorphous structure across the full composition range, while X-ray reflectometry is used to verify film thicknesses. The room-temperature sheet resistance ranges from 110 to 1990 $Ω/\square$, corresponding to resistivities of 125-478 $μΩ\cdot$cm. The sheet resistance generally increases with increasing Si content and decreasing film thickness, although the coupled variation of composition and thickness limits the quantitative separation of these effects. Additional measurements on thicker reference films show superconducting transition temperatures between 5.2 and 7.7 K, with $T_c$ decreasing monotonically at higher Si contents. A comparison of films on different substrate types shows no systematic difference between Si/SiO$_2$ and Si/Si$_3$N$_4$, while films on Al$_2$O$_3$ exhibit elevated sheet resistance at the lower thicknesses. TL;DR: 本文描述了一条用大语言模型（LLM）从已发表文献中提取超导材料数据的流水线，并通过多层“闸门”过滤逐字段提取结果，最终生成一个每条记录都附带来源句子的、可核查的超导材料数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导材料数据以测量结果的形式散落在文献的正文、表格和图片中，收集这些数据通常需要逐篇阅读论文。虽然LLM能够自动阅读论文并构建数据库，但难点在于“信任”：从句子中提取出的数值可能属于相邻化合物，计算得到的转变温度可能未标明是计算值，且论文本身也可能出错。因此需要一种既能自动提取、又能对每个数值进行溯源核查的方法。

Method: 作者提出一条提取流水线：先用LLM从文献中提出超导材料记录（19,576条），再用级联式“闸门”对659,252个字段级提取结果逐一核查过滤。其中644,438个因缺少逐字（verbatim）证据而被拒绝，13,121个因文本未将数值与材料关联而被拒绝，1,693个因来源句子读起来像是计算/预测而非测量而被拒绝。最终保留了3,137条材料记录，每条都附有其数值来源的原始句子。

Result: 流水线共提出19,576条记录；级联闸门拒绝了659,252个字段级提取结果（644,438个缺逐字证据、13,121个数值与材料脱钩、1,693个疑似计算值）。最终数据库存有3,137条材料记录，每条带来源句子。对按数据库比例抽取的500条记录由两个独立模型审计，结果显示：93.4%的受审记录中数值确实出现在文本中且文本确实讨论该记录所命名的化合物，91.8%的记录是测量值而非预测值，说明闸门有效移除了机械性错误。

Conclusion: 通过LLM提取并配合级联式、逐条溯源核查的“闸门”机制，可以构建一个高可信度的超导材料数据库：过滤后绝大多数记录既有文本出处又确实是测量值，证明这种“提取+核查”流水线能够有效消除机械性错误，但文章也指出需进一步探讨这种核查能覆盖到何种程度。

Abstract: The data on superconducting materials are published as measurements and scattered across the literature, in prose, in tables and in figures, so that gathering them into one place means reading the papers themselves. Large language models (LLMs) can read those papers, and databases built that way are appearing. The difficulty is not the reading but the trust: a number lifted out of a sentence can belong to a neighbouring compound, a computed transition temperature can be recorded without saying that it is computed, and the paper itself may be wrong. This paper describes a pipeline that extracts superconducting materials from the published literature with an LLM, checks every value against its source, and asks how far that checking goes. The pipeline proposed 19,576 records; a cascade of gates rejected 659,252 field-level extractions, 644,438 of them for want of verbatim evidence, 13,121 because the text does not attach the value to the material, and 1,693 because the source sentence reads as a calculation. The database that survives holds 3,137 material records, each with the sentence its value came from. An audit of the survivors by two independent models, on 500 records drawn in proportion to the database, shows that the gates remove the errors that are mechanical: in 93.4% of the audited records the value is in the text and the text is about the compound the record names, and 91.8% of them are measurements rather than predictions.

</details>

Abstract: The transport properties of type-II superconductors in the mixed state below the superconducting transition temperature, $T_c$, remain difficult to interpret because superconducting and dissipative processes coexist in the presence of a magnetic field. By combining the longitudinal response with the antisymmetrized Hall signal, we use a phenomenological current-partition model to estimate the dissipative and superconducting current contributions. From this analysis, we estimate the field-dependent superconducting current together with the Hall-derived effective dissipative carrier density and mobility. We also observe an even-in-field transverse signal whose magnitude depends strongly on the driving current. We discuss this signal as a possible mixed-state response involving the magnetic and supercurrent texture of Abrikosov vortices, vortex dynamics, and Cooper-pair current redistribution. The present measurements do not uniquely distinguish vortex, condensate, or combined contributions. The model provides a practical way to compare the longitudinal and transverse mixed-state responses without implying a direct spatial separation of the two current channels.

TL;DR: 本文构造了偶数个非相互作用费米子在一维单位环面上的一类半显式反例，说明激发态的 Hohenberg–Kohn 定理不成立：存在一族势函数，其第一激发态粒子密度恒为常数，从而势到密度的映射不是一对一的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hohenberg–Kohn 定理是密度泛函理论的基石，但其对激发态(以及无相互作用粒子)是否成立长期存在疑问。本文旨在通过构造明确的半显式反例，证明在一维环面上、偶数个非相互作用费米子的情形下，激发态的势到密度映射并非一一对应，从而澄清该定理在激发态情形下的失效。

Method: 利用 Lamé 方程(与 Jacobi 椭圆函数 sn² 相关)的有限带隙性质，取重新缩放的 Lamé 势 v*(x)=2mℓ²sn²(ℓx;m)。通过该势对应的本征函数构造，证明其第一激发能为简单本征值，且第一激发态的粒子密度在环面上为常数。再对该势作平移，得到非平凡的一参数势族，它们都具有相同(常数)的第一激发态粒子密度。对奇数 n≥3 的情形，在谱非共振假设下利用类似方法得到非唯一性。

Result: 对每个偶数 n=2l，存在常数 m∈(0,1) 与 ℓ>0，使得具有重缩放 Lamé 势的 n 体系统具有简单的第一激发能量，且第一激发态粒子密度恒为常数；平移该势得到一个非平凡的一参数势族，其第一激发态密度均相同，故势到密度的映射不是一对一的。在同一谱非共振假设下，对奇数 n≥3 也可得到简单激发能量处的非唯一性。

Conclusion: Hohenberg–Kohn 定理对激发态并不普遍成立：本文给出了明确的半显式反例，展示了势到密度映射的非一一性。此外还讨论了 n=2 时与平面弹性体(elastica)分类问题的联系，并提出了若干相关问题。

Abstract: For an even number of non-interacting fermions on the one-dimensional unit torus, a semi-explicit counterexample to the Hohenberg--Kohn theorem for excited states is exhibited. Indeed, for each $n = 2l$, it is shown that there exist constants $m \in (0, 1)$ and $\ell > 0$ such that an $n$-body system with the rescaled Lamé potential $v_*(x) = 2m \ell^2 \operatorname{sn}^2(\ell x; m)$ has a simple first excited energy and a first excited-state particle density which is constant on the torus. By translating this potential, we exhibit a non-trivial one-parameter family of potentials, all of which have a constant first excited-state density. As such, the potential-to-density map is not one-to-one in this setting. Under a spectral nonresonance assumption, we obtain the same nonuniqueness at a simple excited energy for odd $n \geq 3$. We also explore connections to the classification problem of elastica in the plane when $n = 2$, and outline some related questions of interest.

Abstract: By a multiple-scale asymptotic expansion, we derive effective macroscopic models for a large class of tight-binding Schrödinger equations with slow spatial variations. Our second main result is a Liouville theorem: for an arbitrary symmetric spin-coupling field B, the spin-geodesic flow preserves the canonical Liouville volume on its mechanical phase space. Abstract: Path integrals in quantum field theory are commonly approached through finite-dimensional approximations, but existence and regulator independence of the infinite-dimensional limit require separate analysis. For the massive free Euclidean scalar field, we prove convergence of the finite-dimensional covariance operators and normalized generating functionals by a Galerkin argument. The limit is independent of the compatible functional lattice: any sequence of finite-dimensional subspaces dense in the quadratic-form domain converges to the same continuum covariance and generating functional. A direct Minkowski construction is obtained at fixed Feynman regulator $iε$, followed by the distributional limit $ε\downarrow0$. For interacting theories, each truncation gives an ordinary non-Gaussian integral. At fixed perturbative order, convergence follows when the finite-resolution graph integrands converge pointwise and admit an integrable majorant independent of the functional resolution. Numerical results for one-dimensional $φ^4$ theory approach the known first- and second-order mass-gap coefficients, while four-dimensional calculations exhibit the expected mass divergence and logarithmic wave-function renormalization. Thus functional lattices provide a continuum-space approach to path integrals with mathematically controlled limits in the cases considered here.

Abstract: We extend Jones' Baxterisation, which produces solutions to the Yang-Baxter equation directly from the generators of an algebra, to quantum spin chains with medium-range interactions. Abstract: In this paper, we present a variational formulation and structure-preserving finite element methods for non-isothermal liquid--vapor flows with moving contact lines. Based on the dynamic van der Waals theory with gradient contributions and surface thermodynamics, the governing equations and hydrodynamic boundary conditions are derived from a generalized Hamilton's principle for nonequilibrium thermodynamics. Two equivalent variational formulations based on internal energy and Helmholtz free energy are developed, using entropy density and temperature, respectively, as the primary thermal variable. Using the resulting weak forms, compatible spatial operators and discrete thermodynamic chain rules, we construct two thermodynamically consistent fully discrete finite element schemes. Abstract: State update rules and their behaviour in Bell tests play an important role in the study of measurement schemes and quantum information. TL;DR: 本文在拉格朗日质量坐标下将球对称多方气体 Euler–Poisson 方程化为关于壳层半径 r(t,m) 的单个二阶方程，系统研究其点对称性、Noether 守恒、自相似坍缩解的解析结构、壳层交叉判据及临界 Einstein–de Sitter 坍缩的线性扰动，并以计算机代数和二阶拉格朗日数值格式验证全部结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球对称多方气体（恒星/尘埃）坍缩是引力坍缩和奇点形成的核心模型，但现有研究缺乏对基本方程对称性的完整分类，以及自相似/同源坍缩在规则中心和恒星表面处的解析行为、壳层交叉条件和临界扰动谱的系统刻画，因而需要统一的对称性与解析结构分析。

Method: 在拉格朗日质量坐标下将 Euler–Poisson 方程组化为关于 r(t,m) 的单个二阶方程（含绝热指数 γ>1 与熵剖面 S(m)）；完整确定其全部点对称性，考察临界指数 γ=4/3 时的标度对称及其对称破缺；运用 Noether 定理甄别守恒律与 virial 恒等式；构造精确坍缩解并将自相似问题化归为单个常微分方程；利用 Briot–Bouquet 定理证明规则中心的收敛解析展开；在恒星表面建立指数为多方指数 1/(γ−1) 的 Puiseux 展开；针对 dust 推导 Hopf 型折叠律与自由下落壳层交叉判据；对临界 Einstein–de Sitter 坍缩做线性扰动分离得到球 Bessel 剖面；并用计算机代数与二阶拉格朗日数值格式（含自由面坍缩）的收敛性研究加以验证。

Result: 除时间平移外，方程至多存在一个标度对称，唯一例外是临界指数 γ=4/3，此时对任意熵剖面都存在标度对称；这些标度是非等熵系统二参数标度群的熵固定稳定子，并给出 γ≠4/3 时的精确对称破缺量。Noether 定理选出熵剖面 S=Km^{14−10γ}，此时标度是变分的并给出能量之外的守恒律，否则标度只导致 virial 恒等式。规则中心的自相似与同源坍缩具有含自由中心振幅的收敛解析展开（Briot–Bouquet 定理保证），表面处 Puiseux 展开指数为 1/(γ−1)。质量坐标下密度奇点即 r_m 或 r 的零点；对第一类给出 Hopf 型折叠律与自由下落尘埃的精确壳层交叉判据。临界 Einstein–de Sitter 坍缩的线性扰动精确分离为球 Bessel 剖面，指数满足 3κ²+κ−2+μ=0。

Conclusion: 通过对称性、Noether 定理与解析/数值方法，完整刻画了球对称多方气体坍缩方程的结构：γ=4/3 是标度对称的临界点，Noether 对称性仅在特定熵幂律下给出新守恒律；自相似坍缩在规则中心和表面具有确定的收敛解析展开；建立了壳层交叉判据并给出临界坍缩的球 Bessel 扰动谱，为引力坍缩奇点形成提供了统一且经计算机代数与收敛性研究验证的解析框架。

Abstract: Written in Lagrangian mass coordinates, the spherically symmetric Euler-Poisson equations for a polytropic gas become a single second-order equation for the radius $r(t,m)$ of the shell enclosing mass $m$, with adiabatic index $γ>1$ and entropy profile $S(m)$. We determine all point symmetries of this equation. Apart from time translation there is at most one scaling, except at the critical index $γ=4/3$, where a scaling exists for every entropy profile; these scalings are the entropy-fixed stabilisers of the two-parameter scaling group of the non-isentropic system, and we give the exact symmetry defect when $γ\neq4/3$. Noether's theorem singles out the profiles $S=Km^{14-10γ}$, for which a scaling is variational and gives a conservation law beyond energy; otherwise the scalings yield virial identities. Using the Briot-Bouquet theorem we prove that the regular centre of self-similar and homologous collapse carries a convergent analytic expansion with free central amplitude, and we derive a Puiseux expansion at the stellar surface whose exponent is the polytropic index $1/(γ-1)$. In mass coordinates a density singularity is a zero of $r_m$ or of $r$; for the first kind we prove a Hopf-type fold law and an exact shell-crossing criterion for free-falling dust. Finally, linear perturbations of the critical Einstein-de Sitter collapse separate exactly into spherical Bessel profiles, with exponents solving $3κ^2+κ-2+μ=0$. All identities were checked by computer algebra, and the numerical statements rest on convergence studies of a second-order Lagrangian scheme, including a collapse with a free surface.

</details>

Result: 证明每个Frobenius 2-代数都确定一个满足Yang-Baxter方程的R-矩阵。三个示例分别为：（1）辫子幺半范畴中的一对对象；（2）具有可逆对极的Hopf代数；（3）有限维Hopf代数的Heisenberg double的表示。其中第三个例子精确恢复了Kashaev的万有R-矩阵构造，表明本文构造是Kashaev构造的范畴化推广。

Conclusion: Frobenius 2-代数为R-矩阵提供了一般性的范畴化来源；该框架统一并推广了若干已知构造，尤其是将Kashaev的万有R-矩阵构造纳入到范畴化的一般理论中。

Abstract: In this paper, introducing Frobenius $2$-algebras, which are categorical generalizations of Frobenius algebras, we show that every Frobenius $2$-algebra determines an $R$-matrix satisfying the Yang-Baxter equation in the monoidal category of endomorphisms of its underlying object. Moreover, we illustrate this construction through three examples: a pair of objects in a braided monoidal category, a Hopf algebra with invertible antipode, and representations of the Heisenberg double of a finite-dimensional Hopf algebra. In particular, in the third example, we recover Kashaev's construction of the universal $R$-matrix. Thus, our construction provides a categorical generalization of Kashaev's construction.

</details>

TL;DR: 基于对称性分析，MnSiN2和MnGeN2因晶胞中自旋上、下Mn原子通过旋转或镜面操作（而非反演或平移）相联系，被归类为交变磁体（altermagnets）。DFT与QSGW计算验证了这一点，确定最低能量自旋构型为Pna'2_1'，与中子衍射一致。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别并论证MnSiN2和MnGeN2这一纤锌矿型（Pna2_1）磁性氮化物体系属于交变磁体类别，并系统表征其电子结构、磁性、自旋波与光学性质。

Result: 最低能量自旋构型为Pna'2_1'，与中子衍射数据一致。报告了三种反铁磁有序模式允许的交变磁能带劈裂及其对称性归属，给出了自旋波色散、手性磁振子劈裂、奈尔温度以及光学介电函数。MnSiN2同时表现出Wannier型激子（N-p到Si-s跃迁特征）和电荷转移型Frenkel激子（空穴在N-p、电子在Mn-d轨道）。

Conclusion: MnSiN2和MnGeN2是纤锌矿基的交变磁体，其自旋上、下Mn由旋转/镜面对称性联系；理论计算与实验（中子衍射）一致地确认Pna'2_1'基态，并全面揭示了其交变磁能带劈裂、磁激发及激子特性，其中MnSiN2的激子兼具Wannier型和电荷转移型Frenkel型两种特征。

Abstract: Based on symmetry analysis, the Pna2_1 wurtzite-based crystal structure with Mn as a magnetic group-II element, MnSiN2 and MnGeN2 belong to the class of altermagnets because spin-up and spin-down Mn in the unit cell are related by rotation or mirror plane type operations rather than inversion or translation. Spin polarized density functional theory calculations and quasiparticle self-consistent (QS) GW calculations support the symmetry analysis and identify the lowest-energy spin configuration as Pna'2_1' in agreement with neutron diffraction data. The altermagnetic band splittings allowed for the three different antiferromagnetic ordering patterns are reported and symmetry analyzed. Additionally, spin wave dispersions, chiral magnon spltting, and Neel temperature estimates from the mean-field and random-phase approximations are provided, as well as calculations of the optical dielectric function using the Bethe-Salpeter Equation method. MnSiN2 exhibits both Wannier-type excitons with N-p to Si-s transition character and charge-transfer Frenkel-type excitons with hole on N-p and electron on Mn-d orbital character.

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过分子静力学模拟和模拟布拉格相干衍射成像（Bragg CDI），研究了Cu/Si(111)体系中基底双轴拉伸应变向负载Cu纳米颗粒传递的可行性，发现应变传递呈异质各向异性分布，颗粒平均静水应变为负值，为实验验证提供了空间特征依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可利用基底应变作为控制负载金属纳米颗粒变形的途径，关键在于界面耦合能否将施加的载荷传递到颗粒内部，而此前缺乏对此传递可行性的定量评估。

Method: 构建五种静态Cu/Si(111)构型，基底双轴拉伸应变从0%到2%；使用211,288个原子的Cu颗粒负载于约50×50×20 nm的Si衬底（底部1 nm固定），采用Jelinek修正嵌入原子势（MEAM）进行弛豫；通过仅含Cu的局部拟合提取变形场；并由已知复原子散射振幅反演进行Cu(111)的模拟布拉格相干衍射成像（Bragg CDI），映射随载荷变化的投影位移与应变。

Result: 相对于弛豫后的0%支撑态，Cu局部拟合显示异质、各向异性变形：在每一个非零载荷下，颗粒整体平均静水应变为负值，范围从-3.12×10⁻⁶到-8.84×10⁻⁶；上颗粒（upper-particle）平均应变呈非单调变化。模拟Bragg CDI成功映射了随载荷变化的投影位移和应变场。

Conclusion: 原子模拟与Bragg CDI表征相结合，为这一理想化体系中基底介导应变传递的可行性提供了有力的初步证据，并确定了可供实验研究的空间特征；但真实Cu/Si(111)纳米颗粒中的应变传递仍需实验确认并测定其大小。

Abstract: Substrate strain offers a route to control deformation in supported metallic nanoparticles, provided that interfacial coupling transmits the imposed loading into the particle. We assess this feasibility using five static Cu/Si(111) configurations spanning 0-2% biaxial tensile substrate strain. A 211,288-atom Cu particle is supported by a nominally 50 x 50 x 20 nm Si slab with its bottom 1 nm fixed, using the Jelinek modified embeddedatom potential. Relative to the relaxed supported 0% state, Cu-only local fits reveal heterogeneous, anisotropic deformation: particle-wide mean hydrostatic strain is negative at every nonzero loading, from -3.12 x 10-6 to -8.84 x 10-6, while the upper-particle mean varies nonmonotonically. Simulated Bragg CDI of Cu(111), obtained by inversion of known complex atomic scattering amplitudes, maps loading-dependent projected displacement and strain. The combined atomistic and Bragg CDI representations provide strong preliminary support for the feasibility of substrate-mediated strain transfer in this idealized system and identify spatial signatures for experimental investigation. Experiments remain indispensable to confirm transfer in real Cu/Si(111) nanoparticles and determine its magnitude.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cu2ZnSnS4是一种重要的四元硫族化合物，在光伏和热电领域具有应用前景，但其电子结构、带隙、力学与热力学稳定性以及热电输运等基本性质仍需基于第一性原理进行系统评估，以判断其作为热电转换材料的可行性。

Result: mBJ和HSE06方法得到的带隙分别为0.88 eV和1.55 eV；光学吸收起始于0.8–1.0 eV，吸收较强；0–30 kbar静水压下晶格参数减小、带隙增大；弹性常数满足Born稳定性判据，B/G > 1.75表明材料呈塑性行为；声子计算证实动力学稳定；0–1000 K内Helmholtz自由能下降、振动熵增加、热容在500 K以上趋于平台；Seebeck系数始终为正，250 K时达到约310 μV/K的峰值，1000 K时降至239 μV/K。

Conclusion: Cu2ZnSnS4表现出合适的带隙、高光学吸收、良好的力学与动力学稳定性，以及较高的正Seebeck系数，表明其具备作为热电功率转换材料的良好潜力，值得进一步实验和应用研究。

Abstract: The structural, optoelectronic, mechanical, thermodynamic, and thermoelectric properties of kesterite Cu2ZnSnS4 were investigated using density functional theory. The mBJ and HSE06 approaches yield band gaps of 0.88 and 1.55 eV, respectively. Optical calculations reveal high absorption, with the onset in the 0.8-1.0 eV range. Hydrostatic compression from 0 to 30 kbar decreases the lattice parameters and increases the band gap. The elastic constants satisfy the Born stability criterion, and B/G $>$ 1.75 indicates plastic behavior. Phonon calculations confirm dynamic stability. In the temperature range from 0 to 1000 K, the Helmholtz free energy decreases, the vibrational entropy increases, and the heat capacity approaches a plateau above 500 K. The thermoelectric properties were investigated in the temperature range from 100 to 1000 K. The Seebeck coefficient remains positive, peaking at approximately 310 $μ$V/K at 250 K and decreasing to 239 $μ$V/K at 1000 K. These results encourage the investigation of Cu2ZnSnS4 as a good candidate for thermoelectric power conversion.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LPC中，模型中最不确定的部分并非体扩散，而是金属吸收碳的瞬态表面动力学。直接采用"工艺制度-结果"型经验关系缺乏机理可解释性，因此需要一种将机器学习作为边界条件闭合算子的方案，以提升对LPC过程的建模精度与可解释性。

Method: 以偏微分方程（扩散方程）描述奥氏体中的碳扩散，将有效碳通量边界条件视为待辨识的未知强迫项；采用机器学习作为扩散问题边界条件的闭合算子，并利用碳浓度分布、积分质量增重、表面浓度、有效硬化层深度及附加工艺测量等数据进行标定。在理论上将该问题定位为受偏微分方程、物理通量约束、反问题正则化与不确定性量化约束的算子学习问题。

Conclusion: 该混合建模方法可作为开发可解释的LPC过程数字化支持的算力基础，兼具物理可解释性与数据驱动的自适应能力，为后续工程化应用提供了理论框架与算法支撑。

Abstract: This paper formulates an extended framework for a hybrid physically informed data-driven model of low-pressure carburizing (LPC), in which diffusive carbon transport in austenite is described by a physical equation, whereas the effective carbon flux across the gas-metal interface is treated as an unknown boundary forcing to be identified from experimental and process data. The basis for this formulation is that, in LPC, the most uncertain part of the model is not bulk diffusion but the transient surface kinetics of carbon uptake by the metal. In contrast to direct empirical relationships of the 'process regime-result' type, the proposed scheme uses machine learning as a closure operator for the boundary condition in the diffusion problem. This operator is calibrated using carbon concentration profiles, integral mass gain, surface concentration, effective case depth, and additional process measurements. Theoretically, the model is positioned as an operator-learning problem in a system constrained by a partial differential equation, physical flux constraints, inverse-problem regularization, and uncertainty quantification. The relationships between the proposed approach and physics-informed neural networks, operator learning, dynamic-system identification, boundary-operator learning, and boundary-control problems are considered. The mathematical formulation, calibration functional, identifiability conditions, preliminary tuning algorithm, and online adaptation scheme are formulated. The proposed approach is considered as a computational basis for developing interpretable digital support for LPC processes.

</details>

Abstract: AmorphGen is an open-source Python package for generating and analyzing amorphous structures from a composition or a crystal. Chemically informed rules estimate initial densities and assign separation and coordination constraints. Three routes are available: random placement followed by relaxation, melt-quench from a crystal, and a hybrid route that anneals and quenches random structures. ASE provides access to classical and machine-learning potentials, with GPU batching for selected backends. Analysis tools compute coordination numbers, bond angles, rings, and X-ray or neutron structure factors for comparison with measurements. An optional interface to the FEFF code computes extended X-ray absorption fine structure (EXAFS) spectra. For amorphous Ga2O3, TiO2 and InGaZnO4, the cation coordination numbers are close to reference models and measurements, and the X-ray structure factors reproduce the main measured peaks. The EXAFS spectra for Ga2O3 and InGaZnO4 also capture the dominant first-shell features observed experimentally. The hybrid route reduces bonding defects in SiO2 and As and builds the first sharp diffraction peak of SiO2. By simplifying ensemble generation and standardizing structural assessment, AmorphGen makes it practical to compare amorphous models across compositions, potentials, and preparation routes, and to screen new amorphous compositions.

Abstract: Interactions at solid-liquid interfaces are fundamental to hydrovoltaic and catalytic technologies. Abstract: Lithium transport in disordered rocksalt cathodes depends on local cation arrangements and atomic distortions that are hidden from average-structure analysis. Abstract: Electrides and altermagnets are two distinct yet intriguing classes of quantum materials, characterized by interstitial anionic electrons and nonrelativistic spin splitting, respectively. Our results reveal a magnetoelectric effect in altermagnetic electrides, paving a way towards electride-based spintronic devices.

These results highlight horizontal mirror symmetry as a constraint on phonon scattering in corrugated oxides.

*Joe Lunn,Teresa Orr,Megan Leyland,Ayobami Daramola,Rémi Delaporte-Mathurin,James Dark,Graeme J. Ackland,Prashanth Srinivasan,Andrew P. Horsfield,Mikhail Yu. Lavrentiev,Duc Nguyen-Manh,Mark R. Gilbert,Matthew R. Ryder*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔融FLiBe是聚变应用的候选液态增殖材料，但氚的滞留、输运与提取的预测建模依赖于尚未完全解决的化学与界面假设。氚在含锂氟化物盐中可能以氧化态或氟化物结合态存在，并可交换进入HT等分子氢同位素物种，其相对数量与表现输运行为取决于盐组分、氧化还原状态及气-液和盐-壁界面相互作用。因此需要系统评估现有证据，厘清连续介质模型所需的溶解度、有效扩散系数、渗透率、交换与边界传递参数。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统评估熔融FLiBe中氚物种形态与输运的实验证据，重点梳理连续介质模型所需的溶解度、有效扩散系数、渗透率、交换及边界传递参数；基于证据提出"双池"连续介质描述（氧化态/氟化物结合态与分子氢池分离），但不指定唯一微观物种；并区分直接测量量与拟合/推断的有效参数，同时提出源特异性输运数据的报告与比较规范及后续测量需求。

Result: 现有证据支持采用双池连续介质描述，将氧化态/氟化物结合态与分子氢池分开，且在不指定唯一微观物种的情况下仍可适用（前提是未纳入池中的态对氚存量与输运无实质影响）；文章明确了直接测量量与拟合有效参数之间的区别，并给出了约束物种形态、交换、体相输运与边界传递剩余不确定性所需的测量建议。

Conclusion: 熔融FLiBe中氚的滞留、输运与提取建模可采用双池连续介质框架，其中氧化态/氟化物结合态与分子氢池分别处理，无需确定唯一微观物种。为提升模型可靠性，需明确区分直接测量与拟合参数，规范源特异性输运数据的报告与比较方式，并开展针对性测量以约束物种形态、交换、体相输运及边界传递方面的剩余不确定性。

Abstract: Molten lithium fluoride-beryllium fluoride (FLiBe) is a candidate liquid breeder material for fusion applications, but predictive modelling of tritium retention, transport and extraction depends on chemical and interfacial assumptions that remain only partly resolved. Tritium produced in lithium-bearing fluoride salts may occur in oxidised or fluoride-associated states and can exchange into molecular hydrogen-isotope species such as HT. The relative populations and apparent transport behaviour depend on salt composition and condition, redox state, and interactions at gas-liquid and salt-wall boundaries. This review assesses the evidence for tritium speciation and transport in molten FLiBe, with emphasis on the solubility, effective diffusivity, permeability, exchange and boundary-transfer parameters required by continuum models. The available evidence motivates a two-pool continuum description with separate oxidised or fluoride-associated and molecular-hydrogen pools, without assigning a unique microscopic species. This description applies where states omitted from these pools do not materially affect tritium inventory or transport in the salt. We distinguish directly measured quantities from fitted or inferred effective parameters, recommend how source-specific transport data should be reported and compared, and identify the measurements required to constrain the remaining uncertainties in speciation, exchange, bulk transport and boundary transfer.

</details>


### [81] [Synthesis Method-dependent Resist-like Behavior of Liquid Crystalline Graphene Oxide Films Under Electron Beam Irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28754)
*A. Sahakyan,Ghada E. Khedr,A. A. Hovhannisyan,G. Gevorgyan,K. Margaryan,G. Shakhatuni,M. Aleksanyan,Mohamed Aly Saad Aly,N. Nwaji,H. Gharagulyan*

TL;DR: 本文研究了壳聚糖功能化氧化石墨烯(GO)薄膜在电子束辐照下的结构演化与图案化行为，比较了化学剥离与电化学剥离GO的差异，揭示了合成路线和初始氧化态对辐照诱导微结构形成的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对GO薄膜在电子束辐照下微结构演化的机理认识不足，尤其是不同合成路线（化学vs电化学剥离）和初始氧化态如何影响辐照诱导的烧蚀与致密化途径尚不明确，缺乏将合成、结构与辐照行为联系起来的系统性认知。

Method: 采用实验与理论相结合的方法：(1)利用电子束对壳聚糖功能化的化学/电化学剥离GO薄膜进行辐照，研究其结构演化与图案化行为；(2)借助DFT计算支持并阐释辐照机制；(3)对辐照前后的薄膜进行光电/结构性质综合表征；(4)在微观与宏观尺度系统测试薄膜的力学性能，以阐明观测现象的底层机理。

Result: 实验与DFT计算表明，电子辐照通过选择性触发烧蚀与致密化两条路径来驱动GO的可控微结构形成，且该选择性由GO的合成路线和初始氧化态决定；综合表征建立了合成-结构-辐照之间的新关联，并揭示了辐照诱导微结构演化的基本机制。

Conclusion: 无掩模电子束图案化、多尺度表征与DFT建模的整合，在GO薄膜中确立了此前未被探索的合成-结构-辐照关系，为辐照诱导微结构演化提供了基础性认识，并为设计GO基微纳结构材料开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Here, the electron-beam-induced structural evolution and patterning behavior of chitosan-functionalized GO films, specifically those using chemically vs electrochemically exfoliated GO, are investigated. Experimental results (supported by DFT calculations) revealed that electron irradiation drives controllable microstructure formation in GO by selectively triggering ablation and densification pathways, which are dictated by the GOs synthesis route and initial oxidation state. Furthermore, a comprehensive characterization study was conducted to compare the optostructural properties of the present system prior and subsequent to electron-beam irradiation. Moreover, a systematic study of the films mechanical properties across both micro- and macroscopic scales was also performed to elucidate the underlying mechanisms responsible for the observed phenomena. The results demonstrate that the integration of mask-free electron-beam patterning, multiscale characterization, and DFT modeling establishes a previously unexplored synthesis-structure-irradiation relationship in GO films, providing fundamental insights into irradiation-induced microstructure evolution and opening new avenues for the design of GO-based micro- and nanostructured materials.

</details>


### [82] [DeepDOSReasoner: Physics-Grounded Reasoning for Density-of-States Prediction and Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29132)
*Yingheng Wang,Francesco Ricci,Zhilong Wang,Tao Yu,Junwen Bai,Xiangyun Lei,Shufeng Kong,Fengqi You,John M. Gregoire,Carla P. Gomes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: DeepDOSReasoner 是一个物理驱动的深度学习框架，通过原子到能量的源注意力解码光谱，并利用学习到的连续性方程输运来规范化 DOS，从而在保证非负性和总态守恒的前提下，高精度、毫秒级地预测电子态密度（eDOS）和声子态密度（phDOS），填补 Materials Project 大量缺失数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子和声子态密度（eDOS/phDOS）决定材料的关键电子、光学、振动和热力学性质，但用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算昂贵，Materials Project 中约 60% 条目缺 eDOS、超过 80% 缺 phDOS。现有晶体到光谱模型采用池化的结构表示和逐点回归，既模糊了光谱特征，又违反总态守恒。因此需要一种既保真又满足物理约束的高效预测方法。

Method: 提出 DeepDOSReasoner 框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 通过原子到能量源注意力（atom-to-energy source attention）解码光谱，从结构中获得元素分辨的贡献；(2) 通过学习的连续性方程输运（learned continuity-equation transport）对归一化 DOS 进行精化，按构造强制非负性和总态守恒。方法在仅 25% 数据训练时仍保持相当精度，且两阶段缺一不可（消融实验证实）。

Result: DeepDOSReasoner 在 eDOS 和 phDOS 的所有评估指标上取得最高预测精度；无需监督即可恢复元素分辨的贡献；最可靠地识别稀有价带（VB）能隙。在 8,106 种未表征材料的前瞻性筛选中（经新 DFT 计算验证），其预测仍最准确，前 50 名候选中有 25 个被确认为 VB 能隙材料。三个案例研究（取代化合物、光伏硫族化合物、高熵合金）展示了跨材料类别的迁移性。此外，释放了填充 91,405 条缺失 eDOS 和 127,768 条缺失 phDOS 的 Materials Project 预测。

Conclusion: DeepDOSReasoner 将机器学习与物理推理相结合，实现了高吞吐量光谱筛选和自主材料发现的蓝图：毫秒级预测使穷举式筛选成为可能，其物理约束（非负性、总态守恒）和原子可解释性显著优于现有池化表示与逐点回归方法。

Abstract: Predicting a material's behavior from its atomic structure is a longstanding goal of the physical sciences. The electronic and phonon densities of states (eDOS and phDOS) govern key electronic, optical, vibrational, and thermodynamic properties, yet their calculation using density functional theory (DFT) is costly: about 60% of Materials Project entries lack eDOS and over 80% lack phDOS. Existing crystal-to-spectrum models rely on pooled structural representations and pointwise regression, which blur spectral features and violate total-state conservation. Here we introduce DeepDOSReasoner, a physics-grounded framework that decodes spectra through atom-to-energy source attention and refines the normalized DOS through learned continuity-equation transport, enforcing nonnegativity and total-state conservation by construction. DeepDOSReasoner achieves the highest prediction accuracy across all evaluated metrics for both eDOS and phDOS, recovers element-resolved contributions without supervision, and most reliably identifies rare valence-band (VB) gaps. In a prospective screen of 8,106 uncharted materials validated by new DFT calculations, its predictions remain the most accurate among the methods evaluated, with 25 of its top 50 candidates confirmed as VB-gap materials. Three case studies spanning substituted compounds, photovoltaic chalcogenides, and a high-entropy alloy demonstrate transfer across materials classes. Ablations confirm that both stages are necessary, while the model retains comparable accuracy when trained on only 25% of the data. Predicting spectra in milliseconds enables exhaustive screening; we release predictions filling the missing eDOS for 91,405 and phDOS for 127,768 Materials Project entries. DeepDOSReasoner provides a blueprint for integrating machine learning with physical reasoning toward high-throughput spectral screening and autonomous materials discovery.

</details>


### [83] [CLFF: Automated Framework for Concurrent Learning Force Fields and Material Property Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29187)
*Cao Thang Nguyen,Sung Youb Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: CLFF 是一个开源的 Python 框架，用于自动化机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的并发学习工作流，将训练、采样、不确定性筛选、量子化学标注与数据集扩充统一起来，并支持跨异构远程资源的异步分布式计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发和部署机器学习原子间势通常涉及复杂的流程，需要大量人工干预和领域专业知识，因此需要一种能够自动化这些流程、减少人工负担并提升可复现性的框架。

Method: 作者开发了开源的 Python 框架 CLFF，将模型训练、MLIP 驱动的采样、基于不确定性的候选结构筛选、量子力学标注和数据集扩增整合为统一的并发学习工作流；该框架支持多种现代基于图神经网络的 MLIP 架构，兼容主流计算化学与材料科学工具，并能将计算任务异步分布到多个异构远程计算资源上。此外还提供初始数据生成、声子谱和势能面扫描等物性计算的自动化工作流。

Result: CLFF 成功实现了 MLIP 并发学习全流程的自动化，支持多种图神经网络势架构，能够异步调度跨异构远程资源的计算任务，聚合碎片化的硬件基础设施于单一可控工作流中，并提供数据生成与物性计算（声子色散、势能面扫描）的自动化功能。

Conclusion: CLFF 通过最小化人工干预并增强可复现性，为计算材料研究中机器学习原子间势的开发与部署提供了一个灵活的平台。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) offer an efficient paradigm for large-scale atomistic simulations with near first-principles accuracy. However, developing and deploying these potentials often involves intricate procedures that require substantial manual effort and domain expertise. In this work, we present CLFF, an open-source Python framework designed to automate concurrent learning workflows for MLIPs. CLFF integrates model training, MLIP-driven sampling, uncertainty-based candidate selection, quantum-mechanical labeling, and dataset augmentation into a unified workflow. The framework currently supports several modern graph-based MLIP architectures and is compatible with widely used computational chemistry and materials science tools. A distinguishing feature of CLFF is its ability to asynchronously distribute computational tasks across multiple heterogeneous remote computing resources, enabling users to aggregate fragmented hardware infrastructure within a single controllable workflow. In addition to concurrent learning, CLFF provides automated workflows for initial data generation and material property calculations, including phonon dispersion and potential energy surface scans. By minimizing manual intervention and enhancing reproducibility, CLFF offers a flexible platform for developing and deploying MLIPs in computational materials research.

</details>


### [84] [Gallium at germanium surfaces: links to superconducting films and correlated adatom lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30809)
*Peter Jacobson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了锗(001)上外延生长的镓超掺杂锗薄膜的超导性研究，重点探讨镓在Ge(001)和Ge(111)表面占据的晶格位置及其进入生长薄膜的机制，并指出关键位点归属问题尚未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早前离子注入的薄膜中富含镓的析出物模糊了超导性的来源，因此需要厘清在无析出物的外延薄膜中，超导性是否源于替位式镓（受主）所提供的空穴；同时需确定镓在Ge(001)和Ge(111)上的具体占位，以判断其能否像B为Si(111)上Sn超导晶格供穴那样提供空穴。

Method: 通过综述对比研究：包括扫描隧道显微镜（STM）和X射线驻波（XSW）对Ge(111)上Ga位点的测定；将Ga在Ge(001)/Ge(111)上的行为与Ga在Si(001)/Si(111)上，以及In、Sn、Pb、Sb、P在Ge(001)上的行为，乃至元素态和二维Ga进行比较；分析Ga的配位数（表面三配位 vs. 替位四配位）与受主行为之间的关系。

Result: 在已确定的表面配位中，Ga呈三键配位，而薄膜中替位式Ga为四配位并充当受主。STM和XSW已确定Ga在Ge(111)上的位点，但Ge(001)（薄膜实际生长面）上稀Ga的位点尚未被测量。Ga生长通量所预期的Ga分数为17.9%，但Ga在晶格位点间的分布仍属未知。在Ge(111)上，衬底中的Ga可能向关联的Sn吸附原子晶格提供空穴。

Conclusion: Ga能否提供空穴取决于其所处位置及配位数（三配位或四配位）。在Ge(001)和Ge(111)两个表面上，亟需元素特异性测量，并结合吸附与替位的理论计算，以最终确定尚未明确的Ga占位归属，进而判定超导性的空穴来源。

Abstract: Epitaxial films grown on Ge(001) show that germanium hyperdoped with substitutional Ga is superconducting. In earlier implanted films, Ga-rich precipitates obscured the origin of the superconductivity. This review examines the sites Ga occupies on Ge(001) and Ge(111) and how it enters the growing film. The Ga fraction expected from the growth fluxes is 17.9\%, yet the distribution of Ga among lattice sites is unknown. Comparisons include Ga on Si(001) and Si(111),In, Sn, Pb, Sb and P on Ge(001), and elemental and two-dimensional Ga. Where its surface coordination has been established, Ga has three bonds, whereas substitutional Ga in the films is fourfold coordinated and acts as an acceptor. Scanning tunnelling microscopy and X-ray standing waves have assigned Ga sites on Ge(111), but the dilute Ga site on Ge(001), where the films are grown, has yet to be measured. On Ge(111), Ga in the substrate may supply holes to the correlated Sn adatom lattice, as B supplies holes to the superconducting Sn lattice on Si(111). Whether Ga can supply holes depends on where it sits and whether it has three or four neighbours. On both surfaces, element-specific measurements and calculations comparing adsorption with substitution could settle the remaining site assignments.

</details>


### [85] [AgenticMM: A Material-Centered Multi-Agent Architecture for Scalable First-Principles Materials Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29248)
*Zhenkun Yuan,Geoffroy Hautier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgenticMM，一种以材料为中心的多智能体架构：每个材料拥有持久智能体负责其完整工作流并保留科学上下文，另有持久协调者分配共享资源、协调通信与跟踪进度。该架构通过可复用的提示词技能（如 DefectSkills）定义工作流，并在五个化学性质各异半导体的多阶段点缺陷计算中验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一性原理材料建模中的多智能体框架通常按计算任务或科学角色来组织智能体，在跨大量材料扩展时会导致材料特定上下文被反复传递、持久化执行困难以及资源协调复杂化。

Method: 提出 AgenticMM 材料中心化多智能体架构：为每个材料分配一个持久智能体，由其拥有该材料的完整工作流并持续保留科学上下文；同时设置一个持久协调者，负责分配共享资源、协调通信并跟踪整体任务进度。架构支持以可复用的、基于提示词定义的技能（如 DefectSkills prompts）作为工作流的模块化定义。

Result: 通过多阶段点缺陷任务验证可行性：五个化学性质多样的半导体材料各自的智能体并发执行宿主电子结构、相稳定性及本征缺陷计算，证明该架构能够有效支撑多材料并行的复杂持久工作流。

Conclusion: 材料中心化、每材料持久智能体加全局协调者的设计，结合模块化提示词技能，能够降低上下文重复传递、简化持久执行与资源共享，是一种可行的规模化第一性原理材料建模多智能体方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent frameworks are attracting increasing attention as a means of autonomously driving complex, long-running workflows in first-principles materials modeling. However, such frameworks commonly organize AI agents by computational task or scientific role. When scaled across many materials, this organization can require repeated transfers of material-specific context and complicate persistent execution and resource coordination. Here, we introduce AgenticMM, a material-centered multi-agent architecture in which each material has a persistent agent that owns its complete workflow and retains its scientific context, while a persistent coordinator allocates shared resources, mediates communication, and tracks campaign progress. AgenticMM is designed to incorporate reusable, prompt-based skills as modular definitions of these workflows. Abstract: Integrating energy storage directly into microelectronic platforms remains a major challenge. Although solid-state micro-supercapacitors (MSCs) have been previously demonstrated, most still rely on liquid or solvent-containing gel electrolytes, which require additional encapsulation and complicate device integration. Here, we demonstrate wafer-scale fabrication of all-2D, fully solid-state MSCs by combining conductive Ti3C2Tz MXene electrodes with a natural vermiculite (VMT) clay solid electrolyte. Both materials are solution-processable and can be independently patterned using microelectronics compatible fabrication techniques, allowing for their integration across 4-inch Si/SiO2 wafers. The resulting devices showcase an active stack of only 28 nm thick, with an areal capacitance of 205 uF/cm2 and volumetric energy density of ~15.5 mWh/cm3. Notably, this work introduces a patterned 2D clay as a solid electrolyte in an on-chip electrochemical energy-storage device, extending the role of layered materials beyond electrodes to ion-conducting components. To investigate device behavior and degradation, we employ non-destructive imaging ellipsometry to map individual layer thicknesses and extract the spatial distribution of MXene conductivity across the chip. This approach reveals localized changes in electrode properties following prolonged electrochemical cycling, providing insight into degradation processes that are difficult to capture using conventional spatially averaged measurements. Together, these results establish a route towards fully solid-state, all-2D energy-storage devices and highlight the potential of solution-processable layered materials for integration into microelectronic systems.

### [89] [Engineering strategies for high-performance self-powered zinc oxide (ZnO) ultraviolet photodetectors: a design-oriented review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29515)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了自供电ZnO紫外光电探测器，按工程策略（而非材料或机理）重新组织该领域，归纳出五类设计杠杆，并指出零偏压掺杂悖论、性能易被高估的陷阱及剩余瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自供电紫外光电探测器可在零偏压下工作，省去待机功耗并抑制暗电流，适合无电池物联网传感。ZnO是理想的紫外吸收材料（紫外选择性、储量丰富、无毒、可低温溶液加工），但纯净ZnO缺乏零偏压工作所需的内建不对称性。由于现有文献按材料或机理组织，设计逻辑被掩盖，作者旨在按工程策略重新梳理该领域。

Method: 作者按工程策略将文献归纳为五类杠杆：①结与内建电场工程；②掺杂与缺陷控制；③极化耦合；④界面/表面与光电化学工程；⑤结构与光管理。并将每类杠杆与其服务的性能指标及带来的权衡进行映射，提出"零偏压掺杂悖论"作为统一视角，最终凝练为连接目标指标、策略与生长路径的决策树。

Result: 揭示了零偏压掺杂悖论——提高载流子浓度会压缩零偏压收集所需的耗尽区，故自供电最优掺杂条件与有偏压情况不同；同时指出探测率、响应速度、有效面积方面的陷阱会导致零偏压性能被高估；识别出三大剩余瓶颈：持续光电导、p型ZnO及报告一致性问题，这些问题本质是缺陷控制与严格测量问题，而非新材料问题。

Conclusion: 自供电ZnO紫外光电探测器的设计可按五类工程杠杆系统化组织，零偏压优化需考虑掺杂悖论；剩余瓶颈可通过缺陷控制与严格标准化测量解决，无需依赖新材料。

Abstract: Self-powered ultraviolet (UV) photodetectors generate a signal at zero applied bias, removing standby power and suppressing dark current, suiting battery-free Internet-of-Things sensing. Zinc oxide (ZnO) is the archetypal absorber for this task: UV-selective, earth-abundant, non-toxic, and processable at low temperature from solution. Operating it without bias, however, requires a built-in asymmetry that pristine ZnO rarely provides. Because the literature is organized by material or mechanism, the design logic remains obscured. Here we organize the field by engineering strategy, identifying five families of levers (junction and built-in-field engineering, doping and defect control, polarization coupling, interface/surface and photoelectrochemical engineering, and architecture and light management) and mapping each onto the metrics it serves and the trade-offs it imposes. A unifying lens is the zero-bias doping paradox: raising carrier density narrows the depletion region that zero-bias collection needs, so the self-powered optimum differs from the biased one. We flag the detectivity, speed, and active-area pitfalls that make zero-bias performance easy to overstate, and condense the synthesis into a decision tree linking target metric to strategy and growth route. The remaining bottlenecks (persistent photoconductivity, p-type ZnO, and reporting consistency) are problems of defect control and rigorous measurement, not of new materials.

</details>

Method: 采用实验引导模拟(experiment-guided simulations)方法，区别于重原子基于波动成像的机理，利用粒子样电子在Cs校正TEM中的弹道传播实现轻原子超分辨成像(SRI)，并在TiHx和H-LaAlO3体系中开展原位(in-situ)研究。

Conclusion: 通过利用新型电子-物质相互作用，该研究为探测氢动力学提供了前所未有的方法，有助于在多种功能材料中建立精确的结构-性能关系。

Abstract: Transmission electrons - with few-picometer wavelengths - can theoretically resolve all atoms in solids. However, constrained by factors such as lens aberrations, Z-contrast dependence and poor temporal resolution, the scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM) fails to tackle the long-standing challenge of hydrogen dynamics on atomic scale. Here, our experiment-guided simulations reveal that unlike heavy-atom imaging - governed by wave-based imaging process - ballistic propagation of particle-like electrons in Cs-corrected TEM enables super-resolution imaging (SRI) of light atoms, achieving a resolution down to 14 pm and a robust hydrogen contrast of ~24%. Our in-situ SRI study not only uncovers polymetric hydrogen aggregation in TiHx and H-LaAlO3, but also inter-site migration, rotational displacement of hydrogen species and collective hydrogen tunneling-induced polarization surge in the oxide. By exploiting the novel electron-matter interactions, our findings offer an unparalleled approach to probe hydrogen dynamics and establish accurate structure-property relationships in diverse functional materials.

</details>


### [91] [MagPot: A magnetoelastic potential generator for molecular dynamics simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29574)
*P. Nieves,I. Korniienko,S. Arapan,A. Fraile,R. Iglesias,D. Legut*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 MagPot，一个用于自动生成 Bethe-Slater 参数集的 Python 程序，为 LAMMPS 的 SPIN 包中的自旋-晶格分子动力学模拟提供随原子间距变化的磁相互作用，从而简化磁弹性现象的原子尺度模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁弹性现象源于原子位移与磁自由度之间的耦合，其原子级模拟需要磁相互作用显式依赖于原子间距的相互作用模型。然而，手工构建此类依赖距离的磁参数（Heisenberg 交换及 Néel 相互作用）既繁琐又缺乏可重复性。因此需要一种自动化工具，从材料物性出发直接生成 LAMMPS 可用的磁相互作用参数。

Method: 开发了 Python 程序 MagPot，利用一组紧凑的材料物性，确定描述 Heisenberg 交换的径向函数，以及双离子 Néel 相互作用的偶极和四极贡献。版本 1.0 支持简单立方、体心立方和面心立方晶格，以及铁磁和反铁磁构型。其核心特点是模块化架构，使生成的磁贡献能与多种兼容的非磁原子间势以可加方式结合。通过状态方程、居里温度、体磁致伸缩、压力依赖的磁晶各向异性和各向异性磁弹性等测试对参数化流程进行了评估。

Result: MagPot 能自动构建原子间模型中的磁贡献，并成功通过状态方程、居里温度、体磁致伸缩、压力依赖磁晶各向异性及各向异性磁弹性等一系列测试，验证了从材料物性到 LAMMPS 可读磁相互作用参数化流程的有效性与可重复性。

Conclusion: MagPot 提供了一条从材料物性到 LAMMPS 就绪磁相互作用的直接且可重复的路径，实现了磁贡献构建的自动化，从而促进磁弹性和磁致伸缩的大规模原子模拟，以及磁振子-声子耦合相关物理现象的研究。

Abstract: Magnetoelastic phenomena originate from the coupling between atomic displacements and magnetic degrees of freedom, and their atomistic simulation requires interatomic models in which magnetic interactions depend explicitly on interatomic distance. This paper presents MagPot, a Python program that generates Bethe-Slater parameter sets for spin-lattice molecular dynamics simulations using the SPIN package of the Large-scale Atomic/Molecular Massively Parallel Simulator (LAMMPS). From a compact set of material properties, the program determines the radial functions describing Heisenberg exchange and the dipole and quadrupole contributions of the two-ion Néel interaction. Version 1.0 supports simple-cubic, body-centred-cubic and face-centred-cubic lattices with ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic configurations. A central feature of MagPot is its modular architecture, which allows the magnetic contribution it generates to be combined additively with a broad range of compatible non-magnetic interatomic potentials. The parameterisation workflow is assessed through equation-of-state, Curie-temperature, volume-magnetostriction, pressure-dependent magnetocrystalline-anisotropy and anisotropic-magnetoelastic tests. By automating the construction of the magnetic contribution to the interatomic model, MagPot offers a straightforward and reproducible route from material properties to LAMMPS-ready magnetic interactions, facilitating large-scale atomistic simulations of magnetoelasticity and magnetostriction, as well as physical phenomena associated with magnon-phonon coupling.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文发现电荷歧化（charge disproportionation）能够作为在层状极性金属 Sr₃Co₂O₇ 中实现巨磁晶各向异性（234.0 meV/晶胞）的新途径，摆脱了传统上晶体场淬灭对轨道角动量的抑制。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属氧化物中晶体场淬灭通常抑制轨道角动量，从而限制磁各向异性，制约了其作为无稀土永磁材料的潜力。因此需要寻找能在过渡金属体系中恢复轨道角动量、产生大磁各向异性的新机制。

Result: 计算表明面内 [110] 磁化比 [001] 磁化每晶胞能量低 234.0 meV。电荷歧化及伴随的极性位移重构了局域钴晶体场，产生不等价位点及不同的轨道层级；在位移钴位点上，面内磁化恢复了轨道角动量，形成强方向性轨道响应，经自旋轨道耦合转化为大磁各向异性能。而在中心对称参考体系中，轨道矩几乎仍被淬灭，各向异性显著减弱；传统二阶贡献很小。

Conclusion: 电荷歧化驱动的轨道重构可作为实现大磁各向异性的设计原理，层状钙钛矿和磁性极性金属可作为寻找无稀土永磁材料的平台。

Abstract: Crystal-field quenching usually suppresses orbital angular momentum and limits magnetic anisotropy in transition-metal oxides. Here we identify charge disproportionation as a route to giant magnetocrystalline anisotropy in the layered polar metal Sr$_3$Co$_2$O$_7$. First-principles calculations reveal an energy preference of 234.0 meV per cell for in-plane [110] magnetization over [001]. Charge disproportionation and the associated polar displacement reconstruct the local cobalt crystal fields, creating inequivalent sites with distinct orbital hierarchies. This reconstruction enables a strongly directional orbital response, particularly at the displaced cobalt site, where orbital angular momentum is recovered for in-plane magnetization. Spin--orbit coupling converts this response into a large magnetic anisotropic energy. In the centrosymmetric reference, the orbital moments remain nearly quenched and the anisotropy is strongly reduced. A local Green-function and orbital-moment analysis connects the large energy scale to orbital unquenching, while the conventional second-order contribution is small within the adopted approximation. These results identify charge-disproportionation-driven orbital reconstruction as a design principle for large anisotropy and motivate layered perovskites and magnetic polar metals as platforms in the search for rare-earth-free permanent magnets.

</details>


### [93] [Asymmetrical transport distribution function: skewness as a key to enhance thermoelectric performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30565)
*Jin-Cheng Zheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了输运分布函数（TDF）的形状对材料热电性能的影响，发现用偏度（skewness）可普遍描述TDF的不对称性，并据此构造了一种新颖的"热电偏度"，能够准确预测热电功率，且增大TDF的不对称性可提升热电性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何实现高热电优值仍是一项科学挑战。由于热电性质可统一写成输运分布函数（TDF）的积分，但TDF的形状（尤其是不对称性）如何影响热电性能尚缺乏系统认识，需要通过TDF形状效应研究建立统计量（偏度）与热电性能之间的联系。

Method: 通过求解玻尔兹曼输运方程，将热电性质表示为单一函数——输运分布函数（TDF）的积分；系统考察多种典型函数形式下TDF的形状效应，定义对称与非对称TDF函数，构造新颖的"热电偏度"，并与第一性原理计算及实验结果进行对比验证。

Result: 发现TDF的不对称性（以偏度为特征）可普遍描述热电性能的变化趋势；提出的热电偏度既能完美捕捉传统偏度的主要特征，又能准确预测热电功率；增大TDF的不对称性可提升热电性能；基于费米-狄拉克量子统计的热电输运性质可由经典统计的形状参数（偏度）很好地描述。

Conclusion: 热电偏度在TDF统计量（不对称性）与材料热电性能之间扮演了特殊的桥梁作用；通过增大TDF不对称性可增强热电性能，并且量子统计下的热电输运性质能够用经典统计的偏度参数来刻画。

Abstract: How to achieve high thermoelectric figure of merit is still a scientific challenge. By solving the Boltzmann transport equation, thermoelectric properties can be written as integrals of a single function, the transport distribution function (TDF). In this work, the shape effects of transport distribution function in various typical functional forms on thermoelectric properties of materials are systematically investigated. It is found that the asymmetry of TDF, characterized by skewness, can be used to describe universally the trend of thermoelectric properties. By defining symmetric and asymmetric TDF functions, a novel skewness is then constructed for thermoelectric applications. It is demonstrated, by comparison with ab initio calculations and experiments, that the proposed thermoelectric skewness not only perfectly captures the main feature of conventional skewness, but also is able to predict the thermoelectric power accurately. This comparison confirms the unique feature of our proposed thermoelectric skewness, as well as its special role of connection between the statistics of TDF and thermoelectric properties of materials. It is also found that the thermoelectric performance can be enhanced by increasing the asymmetry of TDF. Finally, it is also interesting to find that the thermoelectric transport properties based on typical quantum statistics (Fermi-Dirac distributions), can be well described by typical shape parameter (skewness) for classical statistics.

</details>

*Kamyar Barakati,Boris N. Slautin,Aditya Raghavan,Babita Babita,Surya Prakash Reddy,Jordan Marshall,Munireach Nannory,Stephen Puplampu,Alla Slautina,Faith M. Hines,Newsha Javanmardi,Sergei V. Kalinin*

TL;DR: 本文探索能否从显微镜操作员的决策历史中学习奖励函数，以用于自动化实验；提出了多种从对比选择中重建奖励的框架，并用隐藏马尔可夫模型和连续状态模型处理决策过程中优先级的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代显微镜获取、分析和处理数据的速度远超人类操作员决定"下一步测量什么"的速度。要在自动化实验中利用这一速度优势，仪器需要遵循操作员的科学逻辑；而将这种逻辑编码的一种方式是使用融入操作过程的奖励函数。然而，为不同实验显式设计合适的奖励函数十分困难，因此作者探索是否能直接从显微镜操作员的决策历史中学习这些奖励函数。

Method: 将记录的操作员在候选测量位置之间的选择作为其偏好的比较证据，提出并比较多种奖励重建框架：基于物理意义图像描述符的可解释线性模型、同一描述符的非线性函数、无监督图像表示加偏好建模，以及偏好训练的深度核启发模型。结果形成样本上的奖励函数空间场，用于驱动仪器。为评估，作者提出基于留出决策和已知目标生成的决策历史的基准方法。为应对决策历史中优先级的变化，使用隐藏马尔可夫模型为反复出现的选择策略赋予不同奖励，并使用连续状态模型跟踪平滑变化的偏好权重，在真实显微镜候选集上生成的受控序列上验证可恢复隐蔽切换与渐进漂移。

Result: 学习到的奖励函数能够从记录的决策中直接得到，形成样本上的空间奖励场，可作为未来自动化实验的候选目标；基准方法可有效比较各重建算法；隐藏马尔可夫模型能够识别反复出现的选择策略并分配不同奖励，连续状态模型能够恢复平滑变化的偏好权重，且在真实显微镜候选集受控序列上成功恢复了隐蔽切换与渐进漂移。

Conclusion: 从显微镜操作员的决策历史中学习奖励函数是可行的，并为自动化显微镜实验提供了候选目标函数；同时提出的建模方法能够捕捉实验过程中优先级的变化，为智能自动化实验系统编码操作员的科学逻辑开辟了途径。

Abstract: Modern microscopes can acquire, analyze and act on data far faster than human operators can decide what to measure next. Exploiting this speed in automated experiments requires the instrument to follow the scientific logic of the operator, and one way to encode that logic is through reward functions that become part of the operation process. Here we explore whether such reward functions can be learned from the decision histories of microscope operators. We propose multiple frameworks in which recorded choices among alternative measurement locations serve as comparative evidence about the operator's preferences, and we compare several algorithms for reconstructing the reward including interpretable linear models built on physically meaningful image descriptors, nonlinear functions of the same descriptors, an unsupervised image representation followed by preference modeling, and a preference trained deep kernel inspired model. The result of this analysis is a spatial field of reward functions over the sample that can be used to run the instrument. We also propose a way to benchmark these approaches, based on withheld decisions and on decision histories generated with known objectives. We then examine the possibility that a decision history contains changing priorities throughout the experiment. A hidden Markov model assigns distinct rewards to recurring selection strategies, whereas a continuous-state model tracks smoothly varying preference weights. Controlled sequences generated over real microscopy candidate sets show that both concealed switches and gradual drift can be recovered. These reward functions, learned directly from recorded decisions, provide candidate objectives for future automated microscopy experiments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钠在其熔化曲线最低点附近呈现出大量复杂的晶体相，但若干实验观测到的相的原子排布至今未被解析，亟需确定其结构。

Method: 使用CALYPSO晶体结构搜索，结合经并行学习（concurrent learning）训练的Deep Potential机器学习势，在超过160万种构型中进行搜索；随后用第一性原理焓计算、吉布斯自由能比较以及固-液共存模拟验证候选结构的相对稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究为未解析的实验相提供了可检验的结构模型，并揭示了钠熔点最低点附近存在多个紧密竞争的低焓构型。

Abstract: Sodium exhibits a remarkable concentration of complex crystalline phases near its melting-curve minimum, yet the atomic arrangements of several experimentally observed phases remain unresolved. Here, we combine CALYPSO crystal structure prediction with Deep Potential models developed through concurrent learning to explore this structural landscape. Searches over more than 1.6 million configurations recover the previously reported $I4/m$ structure of $tI50$ and identify candidate atomic arrangements for $oC120$ and $aP90$, together with three additional low-enthalpy structures, $tI52$, $oP72$, and $oP92$. The proposed candidates reproduce key features of the experimentally indexed cell geometries. First-principles enthalpy calculations, Gibbs free-energy comparisons, and solid--liquid coexistence simulations reveal close competition among these structures and established sodium phases. Near the calculated reference melting minimum, $tI50$ is favored over $cI16$ and $oP8$, whereas $oC120$ and $aP90$ remain closely competitive, with their relative stability sensitive to model accuracy. These results provide testable structural models for unresolved experimental phases and reveal multiple closely competing low-enthalpy configurations near sodium's melting minimum

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种紧凑型MEMS方案，利用PZT/Si双晶片悬臂梁中的主动压电热开关，从稳态热源中实现高频热释电能量收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热释电能量收集通常依赖温度随时间波动，难以利用稳态/低品位热源；且高频运行受限于热响应速度。作者希望实现从稳态低温差热源中高频、紧凑地收集能量。

Method: 采用PZT/Si双晶片悬臂梁，PZT层同时充当机械致动器（压电热开关）与热释电能量转换元件；通过双输出电极分离热释电与压电信号。结合独立测定的热释电系数与瞬态热仿真，预测并对比实际响应，以识别高频运行的瓶颈。
