Conclusion: 该工作验证了一种紧凑型MEMS方法，可从低品位稳态热源中以更高频率进行热释电能量收集，并指出改善界面传热是进一步提升高频性能的方向。

Abstract: This study demonstrates pyroelectric energy harvesting from a steady heat source using active piezoelectric thermal switching in a PZT/Si bimorph cantilever. The same PZT layer serves as both the mechanical actuator and the pyroelectric energy-conversion element. Dual output electrodes enable separation of the pyroelectric and piezoelectric signals. Pyroelectric energy harvesting is experimentally demonstrated at frequencies up to 110 Hz and temperature differences as small as 5 K. Comparison with the response predicted from the independently measured pyroelectric coefficient, together with transient thermal simulations, identifies dynamic interfacial heat transfer as a key limitation to high-frequency operation. This work demonstrates a compact MEMS approach for higher-frequency pyroelectric energy harvesting from low-grade steady heat sources.

</details>


### [97] [Nonmonotonic In-Plane Thermal Transport in Metal-Embedded Graphitic Carbon Nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30015)
*Wenwu Jiang,Hekai Bu,Wei Qiu,Yong Ni*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习势（MLP）结合均匀非平衡分子动力学（HNEMD），研究了Cu嵌入g-C3N4内禀空腔对平面内热输运的影响，发现热导率随Cu占据率呈非单调变化，并揭示其微观机制为两类声子贡献的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属原子嵌入g-C3N4内禀空腔会改变其局域键合与振动性质，但这一修饰对晶格热输运的后果尚不清楚，亟需系统阐明金属嵌入对多孔晶体材料声子输运的影响机制。

Method: 作者将机器学习势（MLP）与均匀非平衡分子动力学（HNEMD）相结合，对Cu嵌入g-C3N4在空腔占据率全范围内开展平面内热输运计算，并进行频率分辨（谱分解）分析；同时以Ag嵌入g-C3N4作为对照体系验证普适性。

Result: 热导率随Cu占据率呈现显著的非单调依赖：中等占据时降低，接近满占据时部分回升。频谱分析表明，Cu引入的低频振动与g-C3N4本征模式耦合，重构了声子色散并抑制10 THz以下的热输运；随Cu占据增加，空腔周围N原子的集体力学响应增强，20–30 THz频段声子群速度增大，高频热贡献随之恢复。低频抑制趋于饱和与高频贡献递增之间的竞争导致了非单调热导率。Ag嵌入体系表现出相似的占据率依赖与频谱再分配。

Conclusion: 竞争性的声子贡献是金属嵌入g-C3N4中非单调热输运的微观起源；利用内禀空腔的占据为多孔晶体材料中声子输运的再分配提供了一条可行途径。

Abstract: Embedding metal atoms within the intrinsic cavities of graphitic carbon nitride (g-C3N4) modifies its local bonding and vibrational properties, yet its consequences for lattice thermal transport remain poorly understood. Here, we combine a machine-learned potential (MLP) with homogeneous nonequilibrium molecular dynamics (HNEMD) to investigate in-plane thermal transport in Cu-embedded g-C3N4 across the full range of cavity occupation. The thermal conductivity exhibits a pronounced nonmonotonic dependence on Cu occupation, decreasing at intermediate occupation and partially recovering as the cavities approach full occupation. Frequency-resolved analysis reveals that this behavior originates from the competition between two phonon contributions. The low-frequency vibrations introduced by Cu couple to the intrinsic modes of g-C3N4, reconstructing the phonon dispersion and suppressing thermal transport below 10 THz. With increasing Cu occupation, the collective mechanical response of the N atoms surrounding the cavities is enhanced, accompanied by increased phonon group velocities in the 20-30 THz range and a corresponding recovery of the high-frequency thermal contribution. The competition between the saturating low-frequency suppression and the increasing high-frequency contribution gives rise to the nonmonotonic thermal conductivity. A similar occupation dependence and spectral redistribution are observed for Ag-embedded g-C3N4. These results identify competing phonon contributions as the microscopic origin of nonmonotonic thermal transport in metal-embedded g-C3N4 and suggest that occupation of intrinsic cavities provides a route to redistributing phonon transport in porous crystalline materials.

</details>


### [98] [NEP-Spin: A spin-dependent neuroevolution potential for large-scale spin--lattice simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30076)

TL;DR: 作者提出了 NEP-Spin，一种用于耦合原子运动与可变长度磁矩的神经演化势，并展示了其在铁磁/反铁磁材料（Fe-Al、Fe、BiFeO₃ 多铁体）能量、力、磁力及大规模动力学模拟中的优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习势大多未将自旋（磁矩）作为独立自由度与原子运动耦合处理，难以准确描述非共线磁性、磁致伸缩及多铁性等磁-声耦合现象；需要一种能同时描述原子位置与磁矩（含无自旋轨道耦合时的独立旋转不变性、含自旋轨道耦合时的联合旋转不变性）的势函数。

Method: 开发 NEP-Spin 神经演化势，通过局域键张量与自旋张量构造能量表达式，保证在无 SOC 下能量对自旋旋转与空间旋转分别不变、在有 SOC 下对联合旋转不变；用公开数据集（共线 Fe-Al、非共线 Fe）对标能量、原子力和磁力；并应用于多铁 BiFeO₃ 的零场回旋波周期、电场响应、磁场下反铁磁畴的极化变化，以及 279 万/593 万原子的铁电孪晶与惯性自旋动力学（利用 GPUMD）模拟。

Result: 在 Fe-Al 和 Fe 数据集上准确复现能量、原子力和磁力。BiFeO₃ 零场回旋波周期预测为 69.7 nm，中子散射实验值为 62–63 nm；反向施加 20 mV/Å 电场使周期延长至 93.2 nm，零场下施加相同极性位移则为 85.1 nm。在均匀反铁磁胞（固定晶格矢量）中，沿正交基面轴的 6 T 磁场使 Fe 磁矩重新取向，并给出相反的离子极化变化 ΔP_y 符号，第一性原理弛豫复现了两种符号。在 279 万原子铁电孪晶中，回旋波旋转平面相对共享初始平面发生不同程度倾斜并在 100 K、10 ps 内保持差异；10 ps 时传播轴仍在其初始方向约 7° 以内。性能方面，GPUMD 在 4 块 RTX 4090 上以每秒 2180 万原子步推进 593 万原子的 BiFeO₃ 胞。

Conclusion: NEP-Spin 成功实现了自旋与原子运动耦合的高精度、高效率描述，能够准确刻画共线/非共线磁性及 BiFeO₃ 多铁体中的磁电耦合、回旋波和磁场/电场响应，并支持数百万原子级的大规模动力学模拟，为磁-声耦合体系的研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: NEP-Spin is a \glsentrylong{nep} for coupled atomic motion and variable-length magnetic moments. Local bond and spin tensors give an energy invariant under independent spin and spatial rotations without \glsentrylong{soc} and under joint rotations with it. We benchmark energies, atomic forces, and magnetic forces on public datasets for collinear Fe--Al and noncollinear Fe. In multiferroic \glsentrylong{bfo}, the predicted zero-field cycloid period is 69.7~nm, compared with 62--63~nm from neutron scattering. An electric field of 20~mV/Å opposite to the polarization lengthens the period to 93.2~nm, while imposing the same polar displacement at zero field gives 85.1~nm. In a uniform antiferromagnetic cell at fixed lattice vectors, 6~T magnetic fields along orthogonal basal axes reorient the Fe moments and give opposite signs of the ionic polarization change $ΔP_y$. First-principles ionic relaxations at the predicted moment vectors reproduce both signs. In a 2.79-million-atom ferroelectric twin, the cycloid rotation planes tilt by different amounts from their shared initial plane and remain distinct over 10~ps at 100~K. At 10~ps the propagation axes lie within about $7^\circ$ of their initial directions. With inertial spin dynamics, GPUMD advances a 5.93-million-atom BiFeO$_3$ cell at 21.8 million atom-steps per second on four RTX 4090 GPUs.

</details>


### [99] [Indentation-induced polar domain reconfiguration in the charge-density-wave semiconductor EuTe4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30121)
*Hidefumi Takahashi,Yuki Umewaka,Kiichiro Yoshida,Ritsuto Nakamura,Akitoshi Nakano,Takao Matsumura,Daiki Sekine,Masakazu Matsubara,Masayuki Ochi,Shintaro Ishiwata*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在室温下、电阻率约为10 mΩ·cm的电荷密度波（CDW）半导体EuTe₄中，演示了通过局部机械压痕实现极性畴重构，证明无需外部电压即可对相对导电的极性材料进行畴工程调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用电场控制导电材料中的极性畴可解锁基于极化的电子功能，但实验上极具挑战性。局部应变、应力或应变梯度驱动的机械作动被认为是一条无需外部电压的替代路径，然而其在相对导电极性材料中的实验验证仍然有限。

Method: 作者选用CDW半导体EuTe₄（室温电阻率约10 mΩ·cm），利用偏振光显微镜和二次谐波产生（SHG）测量表征其CDW诱导的面内极化畴（呈约90°畴构型）；再用尖端半径约1 μm的针进行局部压痕，观察畴的擦写式变化。

Result: 偏振光显微镜和SHG测量揭示了CDW诱导的面内极化畴，呈现约90°畴构型；约1 μm尖端半径的局部压痕可重构这些畴，在楔形区域产生类似写入/擦除的变化。

Conclusion: 这些结果在相对导电的CDW半导体中确立了局部机械场畴工程，其机制可能同时包含类铁弹（ferroelastic-like）和挠曲电（flexoelectric）效应。

Abstract: Controlling polar domains in conducting materials by external fields could unlock polarization-based electronic functionalities, yet remains experimentally challenging. While mechanical actuation driven by local strain, stress, or strain gradients has been proposed as an external-voltage-free route, its experimental demonstration in relatively conducting polar materials remains limited. Here we demonstrate mechanically induced polar-domain reconfiguration in the charge-density-wave (CDW) semiconductor EuTe4 at room temperature, whose resistivity is on the order of 10 m$Ω$cm. Polarized-light microscopy and second-harmonic-generation measurements reveal CDW-induced in-plane polarization domains with an approximately 90 $^\circ$ domain configuration. Local indentation with a $\sim$1 $μ$m-apex-radius needle reconfigures these domains, producing write/erase-like changes in wedge-shaped regions. These results establish local mechanical-field domain engineering in a relatively conducting CDW semiconductor, with possible contributions from both ferroelastic-like and flexoelectric mechanisms.

</details>


### [100] [Generation and non-thermal annealing of dislocations and atomic-scale roughness by nonlinear surface acoustic waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30160)
*Yuan Xu,Maxim V. Shugaev,Leonid V. Zhigilei*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模分子动力学模拟，揭示了非线性瑞利表面声波（SAW）在铂（Pt）中驱动缺陷生成、结构重组与非热退火的机制，并指出其对近表面微观结构声学加工的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面声波虽被广泛用于探测近表面材料性质与微观结构，但其驱动永久性结构改变的潜力尚未被充分探索；作者旨在厘清非线性SAW引发缺陷生成与演化的原子级机制，及其对表面永久修饰（如声学增强催化活性）的贡献。

Method: 采用大规模分子动力学（MD）模拟，研究非线性瑞利波在Pt(111)与Pt(001)两种表面上的缺陷生成与演化，重点关注非线性波陡化、冲击波形成、应力分布、位错（Shockley不全位错）发射、滑移系激活、交叉滑移与位错反应，以及多次SAW作用下的结构演化。

Result: SAW演化表现出强烈的晶体取向依赖性：Pt(111)上波迅速非线性陡化并形成冲击波，局部弹性能集中且近表面应力增大；而Pt(001)上陡化较弱，耗散可与冲击形成竞争。Pt(111)上足够强的SAW在分切应力接近理论剪切强度时从表面发射Shockley不全位错；冲击波前沿高度局域且快速变化的应力场激活多个滑移系，促进交叉滑移与不全位错反应，形成含不动压杆位错（stair-rod）的复杂位错结构。后续SAW可在应力不足以均匀形核的滑移系上激活位错源，其中一种情形导致Shockley不全位错的连续发射并形成次表面孪晶畴。重复SAW作用逐步降低位错密度，展示了SAW驱动的非热退火。位错与自由表面相互作用产生原子级台阶、台地与欠配位构型，为永久表面修饰提供了微观路径。

Conclusion: 研究确立了非线性SAW可作为非热缺陷生成、结构重组与退火的驱动力，为近表面微观结构的声学加工开辟了新机遇，并可能解释铂表面声学增强催化活性的微观来源。

Abstract: Surface acoustic waves (SAWs) are widely used to probe near-surface material properties and microstructure, but their potential to drive permanent structural modification remains largely unexplored. Large-scale molecular dynamics simulations reveal the mechanisms of defect generation and evolution induced by nonlinear Rayleigh waves in Pt. Strong crystallographic dependence of the nonlinear evolution is observed. SAWs on Pt(111) undergo rapid nonlinear sharpening and shock formation, with localized elastic energy and increasing near-surface stresses, whereas much weaker sharpening on Pt(001) allows dissipation to compete effectively with shock formation. For sufficiently strong SAWs on Pt(111), Shockley partial dislocations are emitted from the surface when the resolved shear stress approaches the theoretical shear strength. The highly localized and rapidly changing stress field at the shock front activates multiple slip systems and promotes crossslip and reactions between Shockley partials, producing complex dislocation structures with sessile stair-rod segments. Subsequent SAW interactions activate dislocation sources on slip systems where the stress is insufficient for homogeneous nucleation, leading, in one case, to sequential emission of Shockley partials and formation of a subsurface twinned domain. Repeated SAW passages progressively reduce dislocation density, demonstrating SAW-driven nonthermal annealing. Dislocation interactions with the free surface generate atomic-scale steps, terraces, and other under-coordinated configurations, providing a microscopic pathway for permanent surface modification that may contribute to the acoustically enhanced catalytic activity observed on Pt surfaces. These results establish nonlinear SAWs as drivers of nonthermal defect generation, restructuring, and annealing, opening opportunities for acoustic processing of near-surface microstructure.

</details>


### [101] [Apparent thermal conductivity governed by interface-injected phonon spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30276)
*D. G. Jeong,Terumasa Tadano,J. S. Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过逐模（mode by mode）解析半无限硅的表面温度响应，揭示了热反射法（TDTR/FDTR）测得的表观热导率 κ_app 究竟被增强还是被抑制，取决于经换能器界面注入衬底的声子谱：短平均自由程权重注入升高表面温度（κ_app 增大），长平均自由程权重注入降低表面温度（κ_app 减小）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热反射技术测得的表观热导率 κ_app 会偏离体值 κ_bulk，但注入声子的非平衡谱究竟如何决定 κ_app 是增强还是减小的光谱特征尚不清楚，需厘清其物理机制。

Method: 对半无限 Si 的表面温度响应进行逐声子模解析，考察声子平均自由程 Λ_ω 与热穿透深度可比时的非平衡输运；针对 Al(70 nm)/Si 结构合成 TDTR 相位信号并进行傅里叶拟合，得到不同界面透射谱下的 κ_app，并将其与透射声子的热流加权平均自由程关联。

Result: 短 Λ_ω 权重注入升高表面温度、长 Λ_ω 权重注入降低表面温度；其机制由注入声子在热化前传播深度（由平均自由程决定）与衬底通过其长平均自由程声子导热能力共同决定。对于 Al(70 nm)/Si，κ_app 约在 93–360 W/(m·K) 之间变化，且 κ_app 与透射声子的热流加权 Λ_ω 相关。

Conclusion: 热反射法测得的 κ_app 不仅反映衬底的热物性，还反映跨换能器界面注入的声子谱；传统的抑制函数分析只能预测 κ_app≤κ_bulk，而考虑界面非平衡声子注入可解释 κ_app>κ_bulk 的现象。

Abstract: Thermoreflectance techniques such as time- and frequency-domain thermoreflectance (TDTR and FDTR) are widely used to measure the thermal conductivity of bulk materials, thin films, and interfaces. When phonon mean free paths $Λ_ω$ become comparable to the thermal penetration depth, a Fourier analysis yields an apparent thermal conductivity $κ_{\rm app}$ that deviates from the bulk value $κ_{\rm bulk}$. Recent studies have highlighted the role of the phonon spectrum injected through the transducer interface, which is generally out of equilibrium owing to frequency-dependent transmission, and have shown that accounting for this nonequilibrium distribution can yield $κ_{\rm app}>κ_{\rm bulk}$, unlike conventional suppression-function analyses that predict only $κ_{\rm app}\leqκ_{\rm bulk}$. However, the spectral characteristics governing whether $κ_{\rm app}$ is enhanced or reduced remain unclear. Here, we resolve the surface-temperature response of semi-infinite Si mode by mode and show that short-$Λ_ω$-weighted injection raises the surface temperature, whereas long-$Λ_ω$-weighted injection lowers it. This behavior is governed by how deep the injected phonons travel before thermalizing with the lattice, which is set by their mean free paths, and by how effectively the substrate conducts the injected energy away, which is limited by its long-mean-free-path phonons. For an Al (70 nm)/Si structure, Fourier fits of synthetic TDTR phase signals give $κ_{\rm app}$ of Si from about 93 to 360 W/(m K) for different interfacial transmission spectra, and $κ_{\rm app}$ correlates with the heat-flux-weighted $Λ_ω$ of the transmitted phonons. Thus, $κ_{\rm app}$ from thermoreflectance reflects not only the substrate thermal properties but also the phonon spectrum injected across the transducer interface.

</details>


### [102] [Atomistic insight into defect-mediated boron redistribution and high-temperature strengthening in TiAl alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30330)
*Xiang Meng,Shuhei Shinzato,Zhiqiang Zhao,Linfeng Yu,Jian Guo,Liangli Chu,Kepeng Ouyang,Shigenobu Ogata,Zhuhua Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过混合巨正则蒙特卡洛/分子动力学模拟（配合新开发的机器学习势），系统揭示了TiAl合金中B（硼）溶质的缺陷介导再分布机制及其对高温变形的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B微合金化虽能有效细化TiAl铸态组织并调控力学性能，但稀B在TiAl中的存在形式、与缺陷的相互作用，以及这些相互作用如何主导高温变形，此前尚不清晰，缺乏原子尺度的系统认识。

Method: 采用混合巨正则蒙特卡洛/分子动力学（GC-MC/MD）模拟，并结合新开发的高保真机器学习势，研究B在TiAl中的占位、迁移与缺陷相互作用，以及在多晶模型中的形变行为。

Result: 硼化物显著硬于TiAl基体，作为脆性增强相，其与基体的界面结合取决于表面终止和堆垛次序；体内间隙B被高迁移势垒束缚于富Ti八面体位，但靠近Al空位时可演化为替位掺杂；拉应力与空位浓度升高会促进B在体内富集；B富集于半共格γ/α2界面并优先分配进入α2相；多晶TiAl中B主要积累于无序区，使900 K、1 at.% B时平均流变应力提高9.4%，同时伴随剪切应变积累减少；B通过局部钉扎阻碍位错滑移并抑制形变孪晶，从而提高塑性变形抗力。

Conclusion: 该研究将溶质再分布、缺陷相互作用与形变行为联系起来，建立了基于间隙溶质的原子尺度框架，为高性能TiAl合金的理解与设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Boron (B) microalloying can effectively refine microstructures of as-cast TiAl alloys and regulate their mechanical properties. Yet, how dilute B exists in TiAl and interacts with defects and how these interactions govern high-temperature deformation remain elusive. Here, we systematically elucidate defect-mediated B redistribution in TiAl alloys and its consequences for their deformation using hybrid grand-canonical Monte Carlo/molecular dynamics simulations augmented with a newly developed high-fidelity machine-learning potential. As a typical product of B microalloying, borides are markedly stiffer than the TiAl matrix and act as brittle reinforcing phases, while borides/matrix interfacial adhesion depends on surface termination and stacking sequence. In the bulk, interstitial B tends to be trapped at Ti-rich octahedral sites by high migration barriers but can kinetically evolve into substitutional dopants upon approaching Al vacancies. Increased tensile stress and vacancy concentration further promote B enrichment in the bulk. B is enriched at semi-coherent $γ/α_2$ boundaries and preferentially partitions into the $α_2$ phase. In polycrystalline TiAl, B accumulates primarily in disordered regions, raising the average flow stress by 9.4% at 1 at.% B and 900 K. This strengthening is accompanied by reduced shear strain accumulation. Simulated microstructural evolution and stacking-fault energetics indicate that B impedes dislocation glide via local pinning and suppresses deformation twinning, thereby increasing resistance to plastic deformation. By linking solute redistribution to defect interactions and deformation, these findings establish an atomistic framework for understanding and designing high-performance alloys based on interstitial solutes.

</details>


### [103] [High-Throughput Screening of Phononic Bandgap in Experimentally Realized Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30344)
*Mohammad Bagheri,Kameyab Raza Abidi,Pekka Koskinen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用通用机器学习原子间势对15万余种材料进行高通量筛选，识别出11,176种具有声子带隙的材料，并进一步发现三类锂金属二元化合物具有低室温晶格热导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有声子带隙的材料能通过限制声子散射实现定向热应用，但传统第一性原理方法和实验成本过高，无法在广泛的材料类别中系统探索这一机制。

Method: 采用通用机器学习原子间势量化声子和热力学性质，并以密度泛函理论结果验证其准确性；对154,879种材料进行筛选，对42,347种热力学稳定且已被实验观测的化合物进行声子计算；对动力学稳定材料进行声子带隙分析，并求解线性化声子玻尔兹曼方程分析晶格热导率。

Result: 识别出11,176种具有声子带隙的材料，其中10,960种为光学-光学带隙，270种为声学-光学带隙；并通过对带隙材料晶格热导率的分析，在三种锂金属二元化合物中展示出低室温晶格热导率。

Conclusion: 机器学习原子间势能够高效、准确地用于大规模声子与热力学性质计算，为系统发掘具有声子带隙的功能材料提供了可行途径，并成功找到具有声学-光学带隙且低热导率的候选材料。

Abstract: Materials with a phonon band gap offer a unique mechanism to restrict phonon scattering, making them promising candidates for developing functional materials for targeted thermal applications. Unlocking this mechanism across broad material classes requires systematic exploration, but first-principles methods and experiments are too expensive. Here, we used the universal machine-learning interatomic potential to quantify phonons and thermodynamic properties. We validated the accuracy of machine-learning-calculated results by comparing them with density-functional theory. We screened 154,879 materials and performed phonon calculations for 42,347 thermodynamically stable and experimentally observed compounds. We then performed phonon band gap analysis for dynamically stable materials and identified 11,176 materials with a phononic gap, including 10,960 materials with optical-optical gaps and 270 with acoustic-optical gaps. We also analyzed the lattice thermal conductivity of materials with phonon band gaps to identify candidates with acoustic-optical gaps and low thermal conductivity. Finally, by solving the linearized phonon Boltzmann equation from first principles, we demonstrate low room-temperature lattice thermal conductivities in three Li-metal binaries.

</details>


### [104] [Strain-Induced Tetragonal-to-Cubic Transition in MAPbI$_3$ from Machine-Learned Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30348)
*Daan Holleman,Luuk Klein Nagelvoort,Monica Morales-Masis,Linn Leppert*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过基于机器学习力场的分子动力学模拟（训练基于 DFT 即时进行），在 300 K 下研究了 MAPbI₃ 对外延应变的响应，预测了 ~0.5% 拉伸应变下的四方-立方相变及带隙蓝移，与实验一致，而静态计算会得出相反结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属卤化物钙钛矿是极具前景的光伏吸收材料，但其性能受环境温度下的动态无序和非谐性主导。常用于氧化物钙钛矿的静态零温“应变体相”DFT 计算忽略了这些动态效应，无法准确预测卤化物钙钛矿在室温下对外延应变的响应。因此需要能纳入温度及动态非谐效应的预测模型，以指导理性应变工程。

Method: 采用基于机器学习力场（在 DFT 上即时训练）的分子动力学模拟，在 300 K 下模拟 MAPbI₃ 的体相和自由站立薄膜模型。通过锁定面内晶格矢量施加实验可及的双轴应变，进行纳秒级长时间模拟，并以 MD 轨迹的系综平均计算结构特征（晶格参数、八面体倾斜模式）和带隙等光电性质，与静态计算对比。

Result: 预测在约 0.5% 拉伸应变下发生四方-立方相变，表现为 c/a = 1 和 a⁰a⁰c⁻ 倾斜模式的消失，与 KCl 上外延 MAPbI₃ 薄膜实验一致。在应变诱导的立方相中，MA 阳离子仍保持择优取向，不同于温度诱导的立方相。体相与薄膜模型趋势一致，表面效应在几个八面体层内衰减。基于 MD 快照系综平均的带隙在拉伸应变下蓝移，与实验相符；而对平均结构做静态计算则预测红移。

Conclusion: 带约束晶格矢量的分子动力学是一种预测有限温度应变相图、指导钙钛矿吸收层外延设计的有效方法，能够正确捕捉动态无序和非谐性对应变响应的影响。

Abstract: Metal-halide perovskites are promising photovoltaic absorbers whose optoelectronic properties are highly tunable. Epitaxial strain offers a route to stabilize desirable phases, tune band gaps, and reduce energetic disorder, but rational strain engineering requires predictive models. Static, zero-temperature "strained-bulk" density functional theory (DFT) calculations, successful for oxide perovskites, neglect the dynamic disorder and anharmonicity that govern halide perovskites at ambient temperature. Here, we show that molecular dynamics (MD) based on machine-learned force fields trained on-the-fly on DFT reproduces the structural and optoelectronic response of methylammonium (MA) lead iodide (MAPbI$_3$) to epitaxial strain at 300 K. We simulate bulk and free-standing slab models across experimentally accessible biaxial strains by locking the in-plane lattice vectors in nanosecond-long simulations. We predict a tetragonal-to-cubic transition at $\sim$0.5% tensile strain, marked by $c/a = 1$ and the loss of the $a^0a^0c^-$ tilting pattern, in agreement with experiments on epitaxial MAPbI$_3$ films on KCl. MA cations retain preferential orientations in the strain-induced cubic phase, unlike in its temperature-induced counterpart. Bulk and slab models yield consistent trends, with surface effects decaying within a few octahedral layers. Band gaps computed as ensemble averages over MD snapshots blue-shift under tensile strain, consistent with experiment, whereas static calculations on averaged structures predict a red shift. Our study positions MD with constrained lattice vectors as a method for predicting finite-temperature strain phase diagrams and guiding the epitaxial design of perovskite absorbers.

</details>


### [105] [Manipulation of Ferroelectric Polarization Under Near-Gap Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30359)
*Hanli Cui,Jian Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究近共振光照射对铁电（FE）序的非接触调控，发现近带隙光可诱导中心对称非极性（NP）相到铁电相的转变，而远高于带隙的光则抑制铁电序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非接触式光控铁电序在凝聚态物理中备受关注，但光激发通常会因加热和晶格损伤而破坏或抑制铁电序；作者希望寻找一种通过近共振光调控、以较低晶格加热和损伤实现可逆极性相稳定的物理机制。

Method: 结合包含位点间密度-密度关联的最小 Rice-Mele 模型与针对 GeTe、SnTe 等真实材料的第一性原理计算，研究沿相变路径依赖结构的激发载流子密度；并进一步采用唯象 Landau 理论和占据约束计算分析相变类型及可行光通量。

Result: 当光子能量低于带隙无载流子激发时，仅发生离共振散射，铁电序趋于减弱；将近带隙略微提高光子能量可诱导中心对称非极性相到铁电相的转变（常规压力-温度相图中无法热力学实现）；而远高于带隙的光在固定光激发量下会抑制铁电序。Landau 理论表明两者分别属于连续相变和一级相变，且这些可逆 NP-FE 相变可在中等光通量下触发。

Conclusion: 该机制为利用近共振光、在降低晶格加热和减小晶格损伤的条件下实现可逆的光稳定极性相提供了一条潜在途径。

Abstract: Noncontacting light control of ferroelectric (FE) order has been receiving extensive interest in condensed matter physics. In this work, we investigate near-resonant light irradiation that induces structure-dependent photoexcited carrier density along the transition pathway. We combine a minimal Rice-Mele model containing intersite density-density correlations with ab initio calculations for realistic materials such as GeTe and SnTe. If the photon energy is below-gap without carrier excitation, only off-resonant scattering occurs and the FE order tends to be diminished. Slightly increasing photon energy at the near-gap regime would lead to a centrosymmetric non-polar (NP) to FE phase transition, which is not thermally accessible in conventional pressure-temperature phase diagram. This is in contrast to the well-above-gap light situation that tends to suppress the FE order under a fixed amount of photoexcitation. We apply phenomenological Landau theory and show that they belong to the continuous and first-order phase transitions, respectively. The occupation constrained calculations suggest that these reversible NP-FE phase transitions could be triggered under moderate light fluence. Our mechanism provides a potential route to reversible light-stabilized polar phases with reduced lattice heating and marginal lattice damages.

</details>


### [106] [Metal-Dielectric Bilayer Supported Nanoporous Silver Substrates for Ultrasensitive Surface-Enhanced Raman Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30536)
*Maryam Naseer,Anastasiia Sapunova,Ali Douaki,Vincenzo Caligiuri,Silvia Dante,Denis Garoli,Roman Krahne*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"纳米多孔金属-介质层-金属背反射镜"（NPMoDM）结构，可显著增强多孔银薄膜的SERS信号，实现飞摩尔（zeptomolar）级分析物的超灵敏检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物传感和生物医学应用中，解析分析物分子的化学足迹至关重要。SERS是一种强大的非侵入性分子检测工具，但需要开发低成本、可调谐且高效的SERS基底，以提高检测灵敏度并将增强波段调节到所需光谱范围。

Method: 作者在多孔银薄膜下方集成一层薄介质层和金属背反射镜，构成NPMoDM结构，利用该结构在薄膜体相中产生更强的近场增强；通过调节介质层厚度可在可见光范围内调谐增强敏感波段；以罗丹明6G（R6G）和对氨基苯硫酚（p-ATP）为待测分析物进行SERS检测。

Result: NPMoDM结构显著增强了多孔银薄膜的拉曼信号，实现了对孵育在基底上的飞摩尔（zeptomolar）浓度分析物的SERS检测，相当于每平方毫米表面仅存在几个分子。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一个可调谐、可扩展（无需光刻）的超灵敏SERS平台，且表面易于接触并可进行分析物功能化修饰，为超灵敏SERS应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Resolving the chemical footprint of molecules in analytes is of fundamental interest in biosensing and biomedical applications. Surface-enhanced Raman Scattering (SERS) spectroscopy is a powerful tool for non-invasive molecule investigation, and there is a continuous effort to develop highly efficient SERS substrates that can be fabricated at low cost and tuned to the desired spectral range of investigation. Here, we show that the Raman signal of porous silver films can be significantly enhanced by integrating a thin dielectric layer and a metallic backreflector underneath the porous metal film. We call this architecture nanoporous metal on dielectric metal (NPMoDM) and show that it leads to much stronger near-field enhancement in the bulk of the film. Additionally, the spectral band of enhanced sensitivity can be tuned by the thickness of the dielectric layer within the visible spectral range. With rhodamine 6G and para-aminothiophenol as target analytes, we demonstrate SERS detection of substrates incubated with zeptomolar concentrations of the analyte, which corresponds to only few molecules present on a mm2 surface area. Our approach introduces a tunable and scalable (lithography-free) platform for ultrasensitive SERS applications, with a readily accessible surface for analyte functionalization.

</details>


### [107] [Femtosecond laser-controlled production of functional titanium niobate layers from TiO$_{2}$/Nb$_{2}$O$_{5}$ powders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30549)
*Gonzalo Gomez-Munoz,Aarón Calvo-Villoslada,Inmaculada Álvarez-Serrano,María Luisa López,Paloma Fernández,Javier Solis,Belén Sotillo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种限制粉床飞秒激光烧结技术，可在无需高温炉处理的情况下，从TiO₂和Nb₂O₅粉末混合物直接制备钛铌氧化物（TNO）薄膜，并系统研究了激光脉冲能量对薄膜成分、相组成和微观结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统钛铌氧化物（TNO）合成方法需要长时间高温热处理，能耗高且效率低。为克服这一局限，作者希望开发一种无需熔炉、快速且可控的替代制备路线。

Method: 采用限制粉床飞秒（fs）激光技术，从TiO₂和Nb₂O₅粉末混合物出发直接烧结TNO薄膜。系统研究了激光加工条件（尤其是脉冲能量）对薄膜形成、相组成和微观结构的影响，并通过全面表征揭示其形成机制，发现该机制与激光诱导反向转移（LIBT）过程具有关键共性。

Result: 通过调控激光脉冲能量，可以获得不同的TNO相和形貌；建立了激光加工条件与TNO薄膜形成之间的关系，证明限制粉床飞秒激光加工可作为制造功能性氧化物层的替代途径。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了限制粉床飞秒激光加工在无需高温炉条件下制备功能性氧化物薄膜的可行性，并揭示了其形成机制与LIBT过程的关联，同时初步讨论了这些薄膜层的潜在应用。

Abstract: Conventional synthesis methods of titanium niobates (TNO) require prolongated high-temperature treatments. To overcome this limitation, in this work, we have developed a confined powder-bed femtosecond (fs) laser-based technique to sinter TNO films starting from a mixture of TiO$_{2}$ and Nb$_{2}$O$_{5}$ powders, avoiding furnace-based approaches. The influence of laser-processing conditions on film formation, phase composition, and microstructure is systematically investigated, demonstrating that different TNO phases and morphologies can be obtained by controlling the laser pulse energy. A comprehensive characterization of the processed films has been performed, providing insights into the mechanisms governing their formation, which appear to share key features with laser-induced backward transfer (LIBT) processes. Our findings establish the relationship between laser-processing conditions and TNO film formation, highlighting the potential of confined powder-bed fs-laser processing as an alternative route for manufacturing functional oxide layers. Tentative applications of these layers are finally discussed.

</details>


### [108] [Electric Field Assisted Charge Transfer Mediated Electronic Coupling in Ferromagnetic/Multiferroic Hetero-structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30598)
*Subho Saha,Sourav Chowdhury,Suryakanta Mondal,Kartick Biswas,Deepak Prajapat,Andrei Gloskovskii,Suresh Bharadwaj,Mukul Gupta,Pavan Nukala,Bhagwati Prasad,Ram Janay Choudhary*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了LaCoO3/BiFeO3双层异质结中，Co与Fe离子经氧介导的界面电荷转移现象，揭示了电场可调控该电荷转移并影响极化和交换偏置，为电压控制磁各向异性器件提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合的铁磁-多铁材料有望替代CMOS存储器件或单一多铁氧化物，因其兼具电场可控导电性和界面处的大磁化强度，但如何选择合适的材料体系仍极具挑战。

Method: 在SrTiO3衬底上生长LaCoO3(LCO)/BiFeO3(BFO)双层结构，通过界面处氧介导的Co→Fe离子间电荷转移引入Co、Fe的多价态和交换偏置；进一步在外加电场偏置下、于低温区增强电荷转移，并观察其对BFO层极化行为的影响。

Result: 在LCO/BFO界面观察到经氧介导的Co到Fe的离子间电荷转移，诱导了Co和Fe的多价态以及交换偏置；低温区电场偏置可进一步增强电荷转移，并影响BFO层的极化行为，证实了铁磁LCO与多铁BFO层间的电子耦合。

Conclusion: LCO/BFO双层中的电场可控电荷转移与电子耦合，使其成为制造电压控制磁各向异性（VCMA）系统的有前景的材料体系。

Abstract: The strongly coupled ferromagnet-multiferroic material could be a better alternative to complementary metal-oxide semiconductor (CMOS) based data storage device or rather lone multiferroic oxide materials because it offers the electric field controlled conductivity and large magnetisation at the interfaces, but choosing a proper system is still very much challenging. In the present scenario, an interionic charge transfer from Co ion to Fe ion via oxygen is observed at the interfacial region of LaCoO3 (LCO)/BiFeO3 (BFO) grown on SrTiO3 substrate, leading to induce multivalency in Co, Fe ion and also induces exchange biasing in the system. The charge transferring between Co and Fe ions gets further enhanced by electric biasing across the bilayer in the low temperature region, which affects the polarisation behaviour of BFO layer. This confirms the electronic coupling between the ferromagnetic LCO and the multiferroic BFO layers, useful for the making of voltage-controlled magnetic anisotropy (VCMA) systems.

</details>


### [109] [Coupled Altermagnetic and Valley Physics in Monolayer VBiSe$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30649)
*Divyanshi Tyagi,Saswata Bhattacharya*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算和Wannier分析，揭示单层VBiSe₃中交错磁性与谷物理的耦合：无SOC时呈现i波交错磁织构，加入SOC后产生112.2 meV的K/K'谷劈裂，且两谷Berry曲率同号异幅，具备应变可调的谷极化电子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索补偿磁性、自旋轨道耦合（SOC）与谷自由度的协同作用，以期在不依赖传统铁磁性的前提下实现对自旋和谷的调控，为自旋-谷电子学器件提供新平台。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算并结合基于Wannier函数的分析；在无SOC和有SOC两种情形下研究单层VBiSe₃的能带结构、自旋劈裂、谷劈裂及Berry曲率；通过轨道分析和有效SOC哈密顿量识别谷劈裂的微观起源；并施加-1%双轴压缩应变考察电子态的应变调控。

Result: 无SOC时，补偿的反铁磁Néel态（相反自旋V亚晶格仅由镜像操作关联）呈现动量依赖的自旋劈裂及i波交错磁织构；计入SOC后，导带态出现各向异性类Rashba自旋劈裂，并产生约112.2 meV的K/K'谷劈裂。轨道分析表明，SOC活性的Se-px,y态与以V-dz²为主的导带态之间的杂化是谷劈裂的关键微观机制。两谷的Berry曲率同号但大小不同，与常规谷霍尔系统的反号模式不同，因此大谷劈裂为通过栅压实现谷选择性载流子占据提供了途径而非谷霍尔效应。此外，-1%双轴压缩应变使体系进入准半谷金属态，具有谷极化电子和非谷极化空穴。

Conclusion: 单层VBiSe₃为研究交错磁性、自旋轨道耦合与谷现象耦合提供了有前景的平台，且其电子态可通过应变进行调控，为谷选择性载流子操控开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The interplay of compensated magnetism, spin--orbit coupling (SOC), and valley degrees of freedom provides a promising route toward spin--valley control without conventional ferromagnetism. Here, using first-principles calculations combined with Wannier-based analysis, we reveal coupled altermagnetic and valley physics in monolayer VBiSe$_3$. In the absence of SOC, the compensated Néel antiferromagnetic state, whose opposite-spin V sublattices are related only by mirror operations, exhibits momentum-dependent spin splitting with a characteristic $i$-wave altermagnetic texture. Upon inclusion of SOC, the conduction-band states develop anisotropic Rashba-like spin splitting together with a sizable $K/K'$ valley splitting of about $112.2$~meV. Orbital analysis and an effective SOC Hamiltonian identify the hybridization between SOC-active Se-$p_{x,y}$ states and predominantly V-$d_{z^2}$ conduction-band states as an important microscopic origin of the valley splitting. The two valleys carry Berry curvature of the same sign but different magnitude, unlike the opposite-sign pattern of conventional valley Hall systems, so the large valley splitting, rather than a valley Hall effect, offers a possible route to valley-selective carrier occupation by gating. Moreover, a modest $-1\%$ biaxial compressive strain drives the system into a quasi-half-valley-metallic state with valley-polarized electrons and non-valley-polarized holes. These results establish monolayer VBiSe$_3$ as a promising platform for coupled altermagnetic, spin--orbit, and valley phenomena with strain-tunable electronic states.

</details>


### [110] [The effect of isotopically-enriched 70Ge on the Raman scattering in Cr2Ge2Te6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30688)
*Grzegorz Krasucki,Katarzyna Olkowska-Pucko,Tomasz Woźniak,Mihai I. Sturza,Holger Kohlmann,Adam Babiński,Maciej R. Molas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过同位素富集^70Ge的实验证明，Cr2Ge2Te6（CGT）中A5g拉曼模式的尖锐多峰结构源自锗的天然同位素分布，而非其他原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前CGT最高能A5g拉曼模式呈现由一系列极窄峰组成的独特线形，被推测源于Ge同位素分布对Ge-Ge赝分子振动的影响，但一直缺乏直接实验证据。作者旨在通过同位素富集实验对这一假设进行验证。

Method: 对CGT进行^70Ge同位素富集处理，开展拉曼光谱研究（室温与低温测量），并结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，比较富集前后E5g和A5g模式的频移、峰宽及峰形变化。

Result: 同位素富集后，E5g与A5g模式均发生显著蓝移；室温下A5g峰明显变窄，低温测量中原来的多峰结构变为单一的清晰谱线。实验结果与DFT计算精确吻合，证实多峰线形源于Ge同位素分布。

Conclusion: 同位素诱导的拉曼分裂是CGT中A5g多峰结构的成因，并且类似现象可能存在于其他含Ge或Si的结构类似的层状碲化物中。

Abstract: The highest-energy A5g Raman-active vibrational mode in chromium germanium telluride (Cr2Ge2Te6, CGT) exhibits a unique lineshape comprising a series of remarkably narrow peaks. While this multiplet was attributed to the natural isotope distribution of germanium affecting Ge-Ge pseudo-molecular vibrations, direct evidence has been lacking. Here, we present a Raman spectroscopy study of CGT isotopically enriched with 70Ge to validate this hypothesis. Upon isotopic enrichment, both the E5g and A5g modes exhibit a pronounced blue-shift. At room temperature, the A5g peak undergoes significant narrowing, while low-temperature measurements reveal a single, well-defined line instead of the previously observed multiplet. These observations align precisely with density functional theory calculations, confirming that the multi-line profile originates from Ge isotope distribution. Our findings demonstrate that similar isotope-induced Raman splitting may occur in other structurally analogous layered tellurides containing Ge or Si.

</details>


### [111] [Janus Asymmetry Unlocks Rashba Spintronics and Chiral Phononics in Two-Dimensional Carbo-Chalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30767)
*Valentina Barreca,Giorgio Sangiovanni,Giovanni Cantele,Ludovica Zullo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理计算表明，Janus 不对称性可在过渡金属碳硫族化合物（JTMCCs）二维平台中同时激发巨大的 Rashba 自旋劈裂（如 Ta₂CSSe 和 NbTaCS₂ 中 α_R≈2 eV Å）与手性声子劈裂（最大约 28 cm⁻¹），并能诱导原本非手性的碳振动变为手性，且外电场可共同调控自旋与晶格角动量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋电子学与手性声子学为纳米尺度调控提供了互补途径，但在同一二维平台上同时实现两种功能仍具挑战。作者希望利用 Janus 不对称性这一统一策略，在单一二维材料中同时驱动电子自旋轨道耦合效应与手性声子效应。

Method: 采用大规模第一性原理计算，研究过渡金属碳硫族化合物（JTMCCs）中沿两种化学上不同的 Janus 路径（硫族元素取代或过渡金属取代）所诱导的不对称性，定量计算 Rashba 参数 α_R、反向手性声子的劈裂大小，并分析外电场对二者的调控能力。

Result: 两条 Janus 路径均产生巨大 Rashba 响应，Ta₂CSSe 和 NbTaCS₂ 中 α_R≈2 eV Å，超过 Mo/W 基 Janus 过渡金属二硫族化合物的报道值；Janus 不对称性解除反向手性声子的简并，劈裂最大约 28 cm⁻¹；更重要的是，不对称性使中心对称母体化合物中原本非手性的碳振动变为手性，劈裂约 8 cm⁻¹；外电场可同时调控 Rashba 强度与手性声子劈裂。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种在二维材料中统一实现自旋电子学与手性声子学的策略，Janus 不对称性同时驱动巨大 Rashba 效应与手性声子劈裂，外电场提供共同调控旋钮，为二维多功能器件设计奠定基础。

Abstract: Spintronics and chiral phononics offer complementary routes for nanoscale control, yet realizing both functionalities within a common two-dimensional platform remains challenging. Here, using extensive first-principles calculations, we show that Janus asymmetry simultaneously drives both effects in transition-metal carbo-chalcogenides (JTMCCs) along two chemically distinct routes, through either chalcogen or transition-metal substitution. Remarkably, both Janus routes yield giant Rashba responses, with $α_\mathrm{R}\approx2$ eV Å in Ta$_2$CSSe and NbTaCS$_2$, exceeding those reported for Mo/W-based Janus transition-metal dichalcogenides. Furthermore, Janus asymmetry lifts the degeneracy of opposite-chirality phonons, producing splittings as large as $\approx28$ cm$^{-1}$. More strikingly, asymmetry generates chiral carbon vibrations that were achiral in the centrosymmetric parent compounds, with splittings reaching $\approx8$ cm$^{-1}$. Finally, we show external electric fields tunability over Rashba strength and chiral-phonon splittings, providing a common control knob for electronic spin and lattice angular momentum. Our work offers a unified strategy for realizing spintronic and chiral phononic in two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [112] [Generalized dielectric-dependent hybrid functional combining Gaussian and Yukawa screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30930)
*Alexey Tal,Fabien Tran,Wei Chen,Stefan Riemelmoser,Alfredo Pasquarello*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种广义库仑衰减方法（GCAM），结合高斯与汤川型屏蔽函数更精确地描述RPA介电函数。虽然GCAM在动量全范围内显著降低介电函数模型误差，但带隙计算反而误差更大；而在模型BSE光谱计算中GCAM系统性优于CAM。这表明介电依赖杂化泛函的成功部分源于误差抵消，而非纯物理原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有库仑衰减方法（CAM）对RPA介电函数的屏蔽描述不够精确，尤其在整个动量范围内存在误差。作者希望改进介电函数的模型精度，探究这些近似方法的成功究竟来自物理上的正确屏蔽描述，还是来自误差的相互抵消。

Method: 提出广义库仑衰减方法（GCAM），通过联合高斯型与汤川（Yukawa）型屏蔽函数来更精确表示RPA介电函数；系统性地对半导体和绝缘体的RPA介电函数以及均匀电子气的Lindhard介电函数进行拟合；并分别在带隙计算（对比实验值）和基于模型Bethe-Salpeter方程（BSE）框架的光学吸收谱计算（对比对角RPA屏蔽参考谱）中评估GCAM与CAM的表现。

Result: GCAM在全动量范围内显著降低介电函数模型误差。然而带隙计算中，GCAM对宽禁带材料的误差反而比CAM更大，对大多数窄禁带材料误差更小。光学吸收谱方面，GCAM在峰位和相对强度上均系统性优于CAM，与对角RPA屏蔽参考谱更一致。

Conclusion: 介电依赖杂化泛函中CAM的成功并非纯粹源于物理上正确的屏蔽描述，而是部分依赖于误差的相互抵消；GCAM虽物理描述更准确，却在带隙上表现更差，揭示了误差抵消在介电依赖杂化泛函中的重要作用。

Abstract: We develop a generalized Coulomb-attenuated method (GCAM) for dielectric-dependent hybrid functionals that combines Gaussian and Yukawa-type screening functions to provide an accurate representation of the random-phase approximation (RPA) dielectric function. Through systematic fitting to RPA dielectric functions of semiconductors and insulators, and to the Lindhard dielectric function of the homogeneous electron gas, we demonstrate that GCAM significantly reduces errors in the model dielectric function across the full momentum range. However, despite this improved physical description of screening, band gap calculations reveal that GCAM yields larger errors compared to experiment than previous CAM approaches for wide-gap materials, but smaller errors for most narrow-gap materials. This counterintuitive result demonstrates that the success of CAM stems not purely from physical reasons, but also from a partial cancellation of errors. In contrast, for optical absorption spectra calculated within a model Bethe-Salpeter equation framework, GCAM systematically outperforms CAM, showing better agreement with the reference spectra obtained with the diagonal RPA screening in both peak positions and relative intensities. Our findings reveal the important role of error cancellation in dielectric-dependent hybrid functionals.

</details>


### [113] [Interface-Engineered Piezoelectricity and Electromechanical Coupling in Two-Dimensional van der Waals Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30974)
*Shivanand Yadav,Raihan Ahammed,Umer Ali,B. S. Bhadoria,Yogesh S. Chauhan,Amit Agarwal,Somnath Bhowmick*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用高通量密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，系统研究了由57种二维单层构建的461种范德华异质结，建立了功函数失配、界面电荷再分布、偶极子形成与应变依赖机电响应之间的材料设计框架，发现界面可作为活性要素调控机电耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异种二维材料之间的界面能够产生单个组成层所不具备的机电响应，但是目前缺乏系统的材料设计框架来关联界面电荷转移与机电耦合性能，难以指导高性能二维范德华异质结的筛选与设计。

Method: 作者采用高通量密度泛函理论计算，对57种二维单层构成的461种范德华异质结展开研究；通过分析功函数失配、界面电荷再分布和偶极形成，并结合双轴面内应变对应变依赖行为的调控；对半导体异质结分别用电荷密度差（CDD）方法和贝里相位（BP）方法独立计算压电系数并相互验证。

Result: 研究发现异质结形成时通过层间电荷再分布产生面外界面偶极，双轴面内应变可系统性调制这些偶极。异质结呈现两种截然不同的体系：金属性界面具有应变可调的界面偶极，半导体异质结则具有明确的压电响应。CDD方法与BP方法计算的压电系数吻合度在10%以内。在选定的24种异质结中，有效双轴界面机电耦合系数最高可达83.35 pC/m。

Conclusion: 界面可以作为控制机电耦合的活性要素，该工作提供了一套计算指导的策略，用于设计二维范德华异质结中应变可调的面外机电响应，为二维材料在机电领域的功能化设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Interfaces between dissimilar two-dimensional (2D) materials provide a route to engineer electromechanical responses that are not intrinsic to the individual constituent layers. Here, we use high-throughput density functional theory (DFT) calculations to investigate 461 van der Waals heterostructures constructed from 57 2D monolayers and establish a materials-design framework linking work-function mismatch, interfacial charge redistribution, dipole formation, and strain-dependent electromechanical response. We find that heterostructure formation generates out-of-plane interfacial dipoles via charge redistribution between the constituent layers, while biaxial in-plane strain systematically modulates them. The resulting heterostructures exhibit two distinct regimes: metallic interfaces with strain-tunable interfacial dipoles and semiconducting heterostructures with well-defined piezoelectric responses. For the latter, Berry-phase (BP) calculations independently validate the charge-density-difference (CDD) approach, which is generally applicable to all representative systems, with piezoelectric coefficients calculated via two methods agreeing to within 10%. Among 24 selected heterostructures, effective biaxial interfacial electromechanical coefficients reach 83.35 pCm-1 . These results demonstrate that the interface can serve as the active element governing electromechanical coupling and provide a computationally guided strategy for designing strain-tunable out-of-plane electromechanical responses in 2D van der Waals heterostructures.

</details>


### [114] [Terahertz detection of magnon chirality in polycrystalline CsCuCl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30992)
*Juan S. Rodríguez-Hernández,Gabriel F. Freitas,Nicolas M. Kawahala,Rafael S. Freitas,Felix G. G. Hernandez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在反铁磁体 CsCuCl₃ 的压制多晶颗粒（晶粒兼具两种结构手性和所有取向）中，5–7 T 场下亚太赫兹磁振子对一种圆偏振光的吸收强度超过另一种 20 倍以上，且无需单晶即可测量磁振子手性；自旋波理论表明这是塞曼频率（为交换增强能隙的 2–4 倍）将各晶粒的进动轴锁定在磁场方向所致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体中磁振子进动的手性通常只能在单晶上测量，这限制了其研究与应用；作者希望探索能否在无需单晶（如压制多晶颗粒）的条件下仍能观测到磁振子手性。

Method: 采用手性螺旋磁体 CsCuCl₃ 的压制颗粒（pellet）样品，其中晶粒随机取向并同时含左、右两种结构手性；在 5–7 T 磁场下测量亚太赫兹磁振子对两种圆偏振光的吸收，并用自旋波理论分析塞曼频率（为交换增强能隙的 2–4 倍）对进动轴的锁定作用。

Result: 在 5–7 T 范围内，亚太赫兹磁振子对一种圆偏振光的吸收强度是对另一种的 20 倍以上，说明磁振子并非绕各晶粒自身轴进动；自旋波理论将其归因于塞曼频率足够大（为交换增强能隙的 2–4 倍），使每个晶粒的进动轴都被锁定在磁场方向。

Conclusion: 反铁磁体中的磁振子手性可以在无需单晶的情况下获得，多晶压制颗粒即可用于测量；强磁场下塞曼频率锁定进动轴是使手性显现的关键机制。

Abstract: In antiferromagnets, the handedness of magnon precession is usually measured on single crystals. In a pressed pellet of the chiral helimagnet CsCuCl$_3$, whose grains carry both structural handednesses and all orientations, we found that, from 5 to 7 T, the sub-terahertz magnon absorbs one circular polarization more than 20 times as strongly as the other, which rules out precession about each grain's own axis. Spin-wave theory attributes this to the Zeeman frequency, here two to four times the exchange-enhanced gap, which locks the precession axis to the field in every grain. Magnon chirality in antiferromagnets is thus accessible without a single crystal.

</details>


### [115] [Solute-Aware Stochastic Korhonen Model of Electromigration in Wide vs Near-Bamboo Al-Cu Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31206)
*Narahara Chari Dingari,David R. Heskett*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究将演化的Cu场与一维Korhonen应力方程耦合，在随机晶粒图上模拟宽、窄及近竹节状Al-Cu互连线的电迁移失效。结果表明：阴极受限线中Cu耗尽使成核中位时间缩短1.15–1.20倍；将全部Cu视为可动会高估该缩短效应2.24–2.36倍；Cu超过固溶度时寿命不服从Arrhenius，250–300℃拟合的黑氏定律会高估150℃成核时间最高达2.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前随机微结构模拟固定了Cu的效应，而大多数连续介质溶质模型采用规定结构并假定所有Cu均可动。本文旨在弥合两者缺陷，仅允许溶解态Cu移动，过剩Cu以不动的析出物形式保留，从而更真实地刻画Cu在电迁移失效中的作用。

Method: 作者将演化的Cu场耦合到一维Korhonen应力方程，在随机晶粒图上对宽(10 μm)、窄(3 μm)和近竹节Al-Cu线进行模拟；每种条件生成10^3个晶粒图，在200℃下仅让溶解态Cu扩散，析出态Cu保持不动；统计成核时间与位置、不失效(immortal)比例及黑氏定律参数，并针对阴极受限线与储库端宽线分别分析。

Result: 阴极受限线中，按给定Cu扩散率，Cu耗尽使成核中位时间相对不动Cu缩短1.15–1.20倍；若将全部Cu视为可动，则2 wt%时该缩短被进一步高估2.24–2.36倍，指数保持在2附近。在含储库端的宽线中，不动析出物留下的Cu台阶成为失效位点，若四分之一以上析出物阻挡Al扩散则消除其无Cu不失效性。Cu超过固溶度时寿命不服从Arrhenius：250–300℃黑氏拟合会高估150℃成核中位时间，2 wt%Cu时最高达2.33倍，指数近2。模型在实验时长内定性吻合窄线的阴极侧损伤及宽线的Cu积聚与中线峰值，但无法复现窄线阴极侧Cu峰或更长的寿命。

Conclusion: 将溶解态Cu与不动析出态Cu区分开，对准确预测电迁移成核时间与黑氏定律外推至关重要：忽略该区分会系统性高估失效加速，且在高温拟合、低温外推时因溶解分数随温度变化而产生非Arrhenius误差。模型仍需改进以复现窄线阴极侧Cu峰值和更长寿命。

Abstract: We couple an evolving Cu field to the one-dimensional Korhonen stress equation on random grain maps for wide (10 $μ$m), narrow (3 $μ$m) and near-bamboo Al-Cu lines. Earlier random-microstructure simulations fixed the Cu effect, and most continuum solute models used prescribed structures with all Cu mobile; we join the two and let only dissolved Cu move, the excess staying as immobile precipitate. With $10^3$ maps per condition at 200 $^\circ$C, we report nucleation times and sites, immortal fractions and Black's-law parameters. In cathode-limited lines Cu depletion shortens the median nucleation time 1.15- to 1.20-fold relative to immobile Cu for the cited Cu diffusivity; treating all Cu as mobile overstates this a further 2.24- to 2.36-fold at 2 wt%; the exponent stays near 2. In wide lines with reservoir ends, Cu steps left by the immobile precipitate become the failure sites, removing their Cu-free immortality if a quarter or more of the precipitate blocks Al diffusion. With Cu above the solvus the lifetime is not Arrhenius, as the dissolved fraction rises with temperature: a Black's-law fit at 250 to 300 $^\circ$C overestimates the median nucleation time at 150 $^\circ$C up to 2.33-fold at 2 wt% Cu with an exponent near 2. At the test durations the model qualitatively matches the cathode-side damage in narrow lines and the Cu build-up and mid-line peaks in wide lines but not the cathode-side Cu peaks or longer lives of narrow lines.

</details>

### [116] [From raw magnetoresistance to reliable cyclotron masses: A practical guide to processing quantum oscillation data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28044)
*Josh Leeman,Sudipta Chatterjee,Max Hirschberger,Leslie M. Schoop*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文指出，从量子振荡数据中提取回旋质量时，信号处理选择（场窗口、背景扣除、谱窗）会显著影响结果并引入偏差；作者通过数值计算与 RTe3 的 Shubnikov-de Haas 实验量化了这些效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回旋质量通常用 Lifshitz-Kosevich（LK）公式从量子振荡中提取，用于评估关联强度与拓扑态标定，但这些质量依赖信号处理中的主观选择（场窗口、背景扣除方式、傅里叶变换前的谱窗），可能改变谱特征并系统性偏置提取的质量，因此需要系统揭示和量化这些偏差来源。

Method: 作者通过数值计算与稀土三碲化物 RTe3（R=Pr、Nd、Gd、Tb）电荷有序态下的 Shubnikov-de Haas 实验，系统考察三种信号处理因素：有限场区间用单一有效场表示、多项式背景扣除、以及频谱锥削（spectral tapering），分析它们对谱峰与回旋质量的影响。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 用单一有效场表示有限场区间会低估回旋质量；(2) 多项式背景扣除会衰减并移动低频峰；(3) 谱锥削可减少相邻峰泄漏带来的质量偏差，但代价是降低频率分辨率。实验中质量随场窗口变化可达数十个百分点，背景拟合可造成表观峰移，并发现一个曾被报告为偏离 LK 行为的现象实际分解为两个相邻频率。

Conclusion: 从量子振荡提取回旋质量必须仔细控制并报告信号处理选择，否则会引入系统性偏差、产生虚假峰移，并可能导致对 LK 行为的错误判断。

Abstract: Quantum oscillations are an essential probe of the Fermi surface in conductors, and cyclotron masses extracted using the Lifshitz-Kosevich (LK) formula are used to evaluate correlation strength and the assignment of topological states. These masses depend on choices in signal processing, including the field window, method of background subtraction, and spectral window applied before the Fourier transform. These choices are often guided by visual inspection, yet they can alter spectral features and bias the extracted masses. We show that (1) representing a finite field interval by a single effective field can underestimate the cyclotron mass; (2) polynomial background subtraction can attenuate and shift low-frequency peaks; and (3) spectral tapering can reduce mass bias from leakage between neighboring peaks, at the cost of frequency resolution. We demonstrate these effects using numerical calculations and Shubnikov-de Haas experiments on the rare-earth tritellurides RTe3 (R = Pr, Nd, Gd, Tb) with a dense low-frequency spectrum in the charge ordered state. The examples reveal masses shifting by tens of percent with the field window, apparent peak shifts produced only by the background fit, and a reported departure from LK behavior that resolves into two neighboring frequencies.

</details>


### [117] [Magneto-ARPES as a direct probe of Berry Curvature and the Orbital Magnetic Moment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30968)
*Seohyun Kong,Bernd Braunecker,Luke C. Rhodes*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出：弱磁场下的角分辨光电子能谱（magneto-ARPES）可作为一种直接、具有动量分辨能力的手段，同时测量材料的Berry曲率与轨道磁矩；作者发展了弱磁场光电子发射的解析理论，证明一阶光电子强度变化与这两个量成正比，并以单层WSe₂为例演示了提取方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berry曲率和轨道磁矩是理解反常霍尔效应等电子输运现象的核心物理量，但直接以动量分辨方式测量它们仍然在实验上极具挑战，因此需要一种新的、通用的测量途径。

Method: 作者发展了弱磁场中光电子发射（magneto-ARPES）的解析理论。对于任意材料，理论证明光电子发射强度的一阶（磁场导致的）变化正比于Berry曲率和轨道磁矩，即使存在自能效应该结论仍然成立。在线偏振光条件下，矩阵元到磁场一阶保持不变，因此强度变化可直接反映这两个几何量。作者以单层WSe₂为例，展示了如何在典型实验中同时提取Berry曲率和轨道磁矩。

Result: 理论推导表明，光电子发射强度随弱磁场的一阶变化与Berry曲率和轨道磁矩直接成正比，且该比例关系不受自能效应影响；对线偏振光，矩阵元一阶不变，使得强度变化可直接作为两者的探针。通过单层WSe₂的示范计算，验证了两个量均可从典型magneto-ARPES实验中提取。

Conclusion: magneto-ARPES可作为一种通用手段，用于在量子材料中以动量分辨方式测量Berry曲率和轨道磁矩，从而为电子输运现象的几何性质研究开辟了直接实验路径。

Abstract: The Berry curvature and orbital magnetic moment are central to the understanding of electronic transport phenomena such as the anomalous Hall effect, yet measuring these quantities directly with momentum resolution remains experimentally challenging. Here we show that angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy measurements in a weak magnetic field (magneto-ARPES) provide a direct route to both quantities. We develop an analytic theory of photoemission in a weak magnetic field and show that, for any material, the first-order change in photoemission intensity is directly proportional to both the Berry curvature and the orbital magnetic moment, even in the presence of self-energy effects. For linearly polarised light, the matrix elements are unchanged to first order in the field, so the intensity change directly reflects these quantities. Using monolayer WSe$_2$ as an example, we show how both quantities can be extracted in a typical experiment. This finding thus points to magneto-ARPES as a general route for measuring the momentum-resolved Berry curvature and orbital magnetic moment in quantum materials.

</details>


### [118] [Symmetry classes of hyperdeterminant wavefunctions: Application to phase transitions between a bosonic fractional Chern insulator and superfluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28276)
*Guan-Lin Lin,Xiaodong Hu,Di Xiao,Ying Ran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了蜂窝晶格硬核玻色子模型中超流相与分数陈绝缘体相之间的相变，利用对称超行列式（Hdet）波函数，结合投影对称群分析与变分蒙特卡罗方法，构造并优化了不同对称类的Hdet态，并从有效理论角度论证该连续相变由具有四个狄拉克节点（构成两个SU(2)_g双重态，即N_f=2 QCD）的涨落SU(2)_g规范场描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该相变通常被认为难以定量研究，且传统费米子部分子构造在微观层面描述不精确。作者希望克服这一局限，通过更精确的Hdet波函数实现对量子相图的定量研究，并澄清该连续相变的有效理论描述。

Method: 采用对称超行列式（Hdet）波函数；结合投影对称群（PSG）分析与变分蒙特卡罗（VMC）方法；构造并优化不同对称类（即PSG的不同表示）的Hdet态；并基于关联的有效理论分析各对称类所描述的量子相与相变的规范场描述。

Result: 不同PSG对称类对应特定的量子相；与传统费米子部分子构造相比，对称Hdet波函数显著改进了微观描述，从而实现了对量子相图的定量研究；论证该可能的连续相变由涨落的SU(2)_g规范场耦合四个狄拉克节点（形成两个SU(2)_g双重态，即N_f=2 QCD）描述，与通常看法不同。

Conclusion: 对称Hdet波函数为强关联玻色子模型的量子相与相变提供了定量可靠的描述；超流到分数陈绝缘体的连续相变可由N_f=2的量子色动力学（QCD）有效理论刻画，而不是通常所认为的描述方式。

Abstract: We study a phase transition between a superfluid phase and a fractional Chern insulator phase in a hard-core boson model on the honeycomb lattice using symmetric hyperdeterminant (Hdet) wavefunctions. Combining projective symmetry group analysis with variational Monte Carlo, we construct and optimize Hdet states in various symmetry classes. Based on the associated effective theories, we show that different symmetry classes, i.e., representations of the projective symmetry group, describe specific quantum phases. Compared with traditional fermionic parton construction, the symmetric Hdet wavefunctions are shown to improve microscopics significantly, allowing quantitative investigation of quantum phase diagrams. We argue that, in contrast with the common belief, this putative continuous phase transition is described by a fluctuating $SU(2)_g$ gauge field coupling with four Dirac nodes forming two $SU(2)_g$ doublets (i.e., $N_f=2$ QCD).

</details>


### [119] [Intertwined Magneto-Structural Criticality Driving Heterogeneous Collective-Pseudospin Dynamics in Sr2IrO4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28395)
*Arnab Singh,Junyoung Kwon,AM Milinda Abeykoon*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用共振X射线衍射、高能全散射和X射线光子相关谱（XPCS），揭示Sr2IrO4中自发结构畸变与磁序参数的平方标度关系，并证实晶格畸变继承底层二维XY型反铁磁序的临界行为，同时观测到压缩指数弛豫和临界慢化等多尺度磁动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-轨道耦合自由度引发的关联量子现象是新兴量子技术与自旋功能器件的基石，阐明这类系统磁性与结构构型相互交织的合作行为，需要对其磁结构与晶格结构进行精确探测。

Method: 在Sr2IrO4单晶上采用互补手段：共振X射线衍射与高能全散射测量静态磁序与结构畸变，结合X射线光子相关谱（XPCS）探测温度与波矢依赖的多尺度磁动力学。

Result: 发现自发结构畸变随磁序参数呈二次（平方）标度，且晶格畸变继承了底层反铁磁序特有的二维XY型临界行为；XPCS显示压缩指数型弛豫及磁转变附近的临界慢化特征，动力学呈现多尺度、温度与波矢依赖特性。

Conclusion: 自旋-轨道耦合与局域结构畸变可能是协调这种非常规动力学的机制，暗示二者在Sr2IrO4磁性与结构耦合行为中扮演关键角色。

Abstract: Correlated quantum phenomena arising from spin-orbit-coupled degrees of freedom provide foundational paradigms in emerging quantum technologies and spin-based functionalities. Elucidating the cooperative behaviors of these systems requires a precise probing of their intertwined magnetic and structural configurations. Using complementary resonant X-ray diffraction and high-energy total scattering measurements on single crystals of Sr2IrO4, we observe that spontaneous structural distortions scale quadratically with the magnetic order parameter. Importantly, the lattice distortion inherits the characteristic 2D XY -like critical behavior of the underlying antiferromagnetic order. X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy (XPCS) reveals multiscale, temperature- and wave-vector-dependent magnetic dynamics characterized by compressed-exponential relaxation and signatures of critical slowing down near the magnetic transition. These findings suggest the role of spin-orbit coupling and local structural distortions as the possible mechanism orchestrating such unconventional dynamics.

</details>


### [120] [Competing magnetic correlations in the square-lattice Hubbard model with spin-dependent nearest-neighbor and next-nearest-neighbor hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28593)
*Rana Imran Mushtaq,Ji Liu,Yinlong Li,Wing Chi Yu,Xiaosen Yang,Ho-Kin Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究同时包含自旋依赖的最近邻跳跃各向异性 tA 和次近邻跳跃 t' 的二维正方晶格 Hubbard 模型，发现四种磁关联相，并揭示两者协同作用对相图的决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体（altermagnets）的自旋劈裂通常来自自旋依赖的最近邻跳跃各向异性 tA 或次近邻跳跃 t'，二者在 90° 旋转下均变号。当晶格没有镜面时 tA 和 t' 会同时存在，但此前研究都是分别处理的，缺乏二者共同作用的系统研究。

Method: 采用约束路径量子蒙特卡罗（constrained-path quantum Monte Carlo）方法，在 U=4t 下研究同时含 tA 与 t' 的二维正方晶格 Hubbard 模型，系统扫描电子填充 n、tA 和 t'。辅以非相互作用磁化率分析和三种晶格尺寸的有限尺寸标度。

Result: 发现四种磁关联类型：Néel、条纹（stripe）、对角线（diagonal）和螺旋自旋密度波（spiral SDW）。相图同时依赖两种跳跃：对角线关联需要有限的 tA 且被 t' 抑制；条纹关联仅在 t' 足够大、克服 tA 设定的对角线倾向后才出现；Néel 关联在 tA 和 t' 都较大时存活，而单独 NNN 模型在该区域给出条纹或螺旋关联。非相互作用磁化率在小 t' 时匹配主导波矢，但 t'≥0.3 时的 Néel 关联来自相互作用。有限尺寸标度显示长程序仅在近半填充附近一致成立，在测试的条纹、螺旋和对角线点，每格结构因子外推为零。该联合行为持续到 U=6t。

Conclusion: tA 与 t' 必须联合考虑，二者共同决定 Hubbard 模型的磁相图；相互作用诱导的磁关联无法用非相互作用图像完全解释，且除近半填充外未观测到真正的长程磁序。

Abstract: Altermagnets have spin-split bands without net magnetization. The splitting can come from a spin-dependent nearest-neighbor (NN) hopping anisotropy $t_A$ or from a spin-dependent next-nearest-neighbor (NNN) hopping $t'$. Both change sign under a $90^\circ$ rotation and are present together when the lattice has no mirror planes, yet they have so far been studied only separately. Here we study the two-dimensional square-lattice Hubbard model with both $t_A$ and $t'$, using constrained-path quantum Monte Carlo at $U=4t$ and scanning the electron filling $n$, $t_A$ and $t'$. We find four types of magnetic correlations: Néel, stripe, diagonal, and spiral spin-density-wave. The phase diagram depends on both hoppings together. Diagonal correlations require finite $t_A$ and are suppressed by $t'$. Stripe correlations appear only once $t'$ is large enough to overcome the diagonal tendency set by $t_A$. Néel correlations survive at large $t_A$ and large $t'$, where the NNN model alone gives stripe or spiral correlations. The noninteracting susceptibility matches the dominant wavevector at small $t'$, but not the Néel correlations at $t'\geq0.3$, which come from the interaction. Finite-size scaling with three lattice sizes is consistent with long-range order only near half filling; at the stripe, spiral and diagonal points we tested, the structure factor per site extrapolates to zero. The joint behavior persists up to $U=6t$.

</details>


### [121] [Hartree--Fock analysis of the bilayer Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28597)
*E. Langmann,J. Lenells*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在半满Hubbard模型（双平行正方晶格）的Hartree-Fock近似下，研究了弱耦合极限下Néel温度与反铁磁能隙比的渐近公式，确定了相图中顺磁-反铁磁临界曲线附近能隙比的行为，并给出偏离半满时的数值相图及关于其结构的解析猜想。


<details>

Motivation: 研究双层（双平行正方晶格）Hubbard模型的反铁磁性质，特别是层间跃迁t_⊥对弱耦合极限下Néel温度、反铁磁能隙比的影响，以及相图中顺磁-反铁磁临界曲线附近的行为，以理解双层结构对磁性相变的作用。

Method: 采用Hartree-Fock近似方法；对半满情形推导弱耦合极限下Néel温度和反铁磁能隙比的渐近公式；分析临界曲线附近反铁磁能隙比的行为；对偏离半满情形进行数值计算得到相图，并提出关于相图结构的解析猜想。

Result: 得到了弱耦合极限下Néel温度和反铁磁能隙比的渐近公式；确定了顺磁-反铁磁临界曲线附近反铁磁能隙比的行为；给出了偏离半满时的数值相图并提出了相图结构的解析猜想。

Conclusion: 在Hartree-Fock近似下，双层Hubbard模型的反铁磁性质可通过层间跃迁t_⊥和弱耦合展开进行定量刻画；相图结构可由数值结果结合解析猜想加以描述，临界曲线附近能隙比呈现特定行为。

Abstract: We study the half-filled Hubbard model on two parallel square lattices with on-site repulsion $U>0$ and with nearest-neighbor hopping specified by a constant $t>0$ within each plane and by another constant $t_\perp \geq 0$ between the planes. Working in the Hartree--Fock approximation, we obtain asymptotic formulas for the Néel temperature and the antiferromagnetic gap ratio in the weak coupling limit. We also determine the behavior of the antiferromagnetic gap ratio near the critical curve separating the paramagnetic and antiferromagnetic regions in the phase diagram. Furthermore, we present numerical phase diagrams away from half-filling, and we make an analytical conjecture regarding their structure.

</details>


### [122] [Observation of nonadiabatic quantum tunneling in a driven correlated insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28980)
*Mingyao Guo,Radu Andrei,Jaena Park,Je-Geun Park,Eugene Demler,David Hsieh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过时间分辨光谱研究关联绝缘体 NiPS₃，揭示了 Keldysh 交叉区（多光子与隧穿竞争的强场非线性区）中光生载流子的微观机制：观测到受有质动力蓝移的多光子共振出现在隧穿主导的吸收通道内，表明绝热性破缺，并被 Hubbard 模型变分模拟定性复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多新兴固态平台（Floquet 工程、非线性输运、高次谐波产生）在 Keldysh 交叉区附近工作，该区域光场足够强且快、能在亚周期时间尺度重塑电子态；然而此区域光生载流子的微观路径仍不清楚，既不能用微扰多光子吸收、也不能用绝热隧穿充分描述，因此亟需实验与理论阐明。

Method: 在关联范德华绝缘体 NiPS₃ 上进行时间分辨光谱实验，系统大范围调节驱动电场强度与频率；结合基于时间相关高斯变分方法的 Hubbard 模型数值模拟，对实验光谱特征进行定性复现与机理分析。

Result: 在 Keldysh 交叉区观测到类多光子共振，这些共振因有质动力效应发生蓝移，并嵌套在隧穿主导的吸收通道内，构成绝热性破缺的清晰光谱特征；Hubbard 模型高斯变分模拟可定性重现这些特征。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了 Keldysh 交叉区电荷激发的微观机制，并指出该绝热性破缺（有质动力蓝移的多光子共振）是调控关联材料强场非线性动力学的关键要素，为相关固态强场平台的设计提供指导。

Abstract: Many emerging solid-state platforms for Floquet engineering, nonlinear transport, and high harmonic generation operate near the Keldysh crossover, where optical fields are sufficiently strong and fast to reshape electronic states on sub-cycle timescales. Despite its importance, the microscopic pathways for photocarrier generation in this regime remain poorly understood, as neither perturbative multiphoton absorption nor adiabatic tunneling provides an adequate description. Here, we perform time-resolved optical spectroscopy on the correlated van der Waals insulator NiPS$_3$ while systematically tuning the drive electric field and frequency over a wide range. In the crossover regime, we observe multiphoton-like resonances that are blue-shifted by ponderomotive effects within an otherwise tunneling-dominated absorption channel - a clear spectroscopic signature of adiabaticity breakdown. Hubbard-model simulations based on a time-dependent Gaussian variational approach qualitatively reproduce these features. Together, our results elucidate the microscopic mechanisms of charge excitation in the Keldysh crossover regime and identify a key ingredient for controlling strong-field nonlinear dynamics in correlated materials.

</details>


### [123] [Finite-size reversal of a multiparticle correction to local spin spectra in a flux-disordered Kitaev honeycomb model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29070)

TL;DR: 本文研究Kitaev蜂窝模型中自旋翻转引起的通量背景变化及其对局域自旋谱的影响，检验单粒子近似是否会错误估计局域谱凹陷的深度，并通过在不同尺寸晶格上的数值计算给出修正的符号与幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋翻转改变通量背景，从而改变马约拉纳费米子感受到的哈密顿量。作者想验证：即使单粒子近似具有正确的总自旋强度，它是否仍会错估局域自旋谱中凹陷的深度，以及这种误差在不同系统尺寸下符号是否会颠倒。

Method: 在冻结的、通量无序系综上，保留自旋翻转后真实的单粒子跃迁能量与相对谱权重，再将谱权重重新标定以匹配总强度；凹陷深度通过从中心强度中减去两翼频率处强度的平均值来度量。针对奇数末态准粒子数且单粒子权重非零的自旋产生态，比较单粒子近似与恢复多粒子权重后的结果，并在24格点及1536、3456、6144格点环面上计算中位配对修正。

Result: 对于这些态，恢复多粒子权重在24格点上使凹陷变浅，在两种1536格点形状及3456、6144格点环面上使凹陷变深。单粒子近似在24格点上高估平均凹陷深度约16-18%，而在6144格点样本中低估33-56%。因此，从24格点外推会在相同无序条件下对四个更大环面给出错误符号的修正。

Conclusion: 单粒子近似对Kitaev模型中局域自旋谱凹陷深度的估计存在显著误差，且其修正方向随系统尺寸发生反转，说明从小尺寸结果外推不可靠，恢复多粒子权重对于准确刻画局域谱至关重要。

Abstract: A spin flip in the Kitaev honeycomb model changes the flux background and therefore the Hamiltonian seen by the matter fermions. Can a one-particle approximation with the correct total spin intensity nevertheless misestimate the depth of a depression in the local spin spectrum? We test this question in a frozen, flux-disordered ensemble by retaining the actual one-particle transition energies and relative spectral weights after the spin flip, then rescaling those weights to match the full intensity. We measure the depression by subtracting the central intensity from the average at two flanking frequencies. For spin-created states with odd final quasiparticle number and nonzero one-particle weight, restoring multiparticle weight shallows the depression on 24 sites and deepens it on both 1,536-site shapes and the 3,456- and 6,144-site tori, as judged by the median paired correction at each of two energy resolutions. For these states, the one-particle approximation overestimates the mean dip depth by about 16-18% on 24 sites and underestimates it by 33-56% in the 6,144-site sample. Extrapolating from 24 sites would therefore give the wrong sign for this correction on those four larger tori under the same disorder prescription.

</details>


### [124] [A Fiber-Optic Platform for Cryogenic Laser-Pump Neutron-Probe Magneto-Optical Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29114)

<details>
